============================================================
31. PROBLEM AND SOLUTION LOG - VERSION 1.19
============================================================

Purpose: one place that records every real problem met so far, its root cause, the fix, and whether the fix is
confirmed. Failed attempts are kept on purpose: they stop the same dead end from being tried again.
Status words: CONFIRMED = a CI run or test proved it; OPEN = decision or work still pending; UNCONFIRMED = fix written, not yet proven.
New entries are added at the end of this section with the next number (P-nn). Never delete an entry.

31.1 Build and CI problems

P-01  CI run 6 (v0.2.1) - KAPT failed: "Provided Metadata instance has version 2.4.0, while maximum supported version is 2.3.0".
      Cause: Room's KAPT processor could not read Kotlin 2.4 metadata.
      Attempt (v0.2.2/0.2.3): added kotlin-metadata-jvm 2.4.21 to the kapt classpath. RESULT: did not fix the root problem (run 7 failed differently).
      Final fix (v0.2.4): replaced KAPT with KSP 2.3.12 (room-compiler moved to ksp). Status: CONFIRMED.
      Lesson: when a processor and the compiler disagree on versions, prefer the maintained path (KSP) over patching the old one.

P-02  CI run 8 (v0.2.4) - Room error: @Database did not provide a list of entities.
      Cause: QuestionBankDatabase.kt listed entity names without ::class.
      Fix (v0.2.5): all 35 entries written as EntityName::class, explicit imports. Status: CONFIRMED.

P-03  CI run 9 (v0.2.5) - Room error: multiple indices with name index_<table>_question_id_position (2 entities).
      Cause: each solution-parts entity declared the same index twice (unique and non-unique).
      Fix (v0.2.7): kept the unique one only. Status: CONFIRMED.

P-04  CI run 10 (v0.2.7) - compileDebugUnitTestKotlin: Unresolved reference CanonicalDatabaseObjects.
      Cause: missing import in CanonicalSqlContractTest; test code had never been compiled before.
      Fix (v0.2.8): import added. Status: CONFIRMED. Result: first green build (run 11, v0.2.9).
      Lesson: code that was never compiled hides simple errors; compile early and often.

P-05  Run 13 (v0.2.10) - build was green but kspDebugKotlin, compileDebugKotlin and testDebugUnitTest came FROM-CACHE:
      schema JSON was not re-exported and tests did not really run.
      Fix (v0.2.11): workflow uses --no-build-cache; wrapper (gradlew) committed. Status: CONFIRMED (run 14 executed everything).
      Lesson: a green build is only evidence if the tasks really executed.

P-06  Missing Gradle wrapper.
      Fix (v0.2.10-0.2.11): CI generated the wrapper once as an artifact, then it was committed. Status: CONFIRMED (run 13/14).

P-07  Room schema JSON could not be committed (ignored by .gitignore, not uploaded by CI).
      Fix: removed /app/schemas from .gitignore (v0.2.6); CI uploads app/schemas/** (v0.2.9); 1.json committed (v0.2.12). Status: CONFIRMED.

P-08  Instrumented run on v0.2.15 (20000 rows) - Gradle failed in 20 s at checkDebugAndroidTestAarMetadata; no test ran, benchmark log empty.
      Cause: my extra androidTest dependency kotlinx-coroutines-android 1.10.2 conflicted with the app's strictly pinned 1.8.1.
      Fix (v0.2.16): dependency removed (coroutines arrive via Room). Status: CONFIRMED (the next CI compiled past this point).

P-09  CI on v0.2.16 - non-blocking androidTest compile step reported 2 errors in ScaleBenchmarkTest (lines 110 and 119): arrayOf of mixed
      String/Double/Int inferred an intersection type for a reified parameter.
      Fix (v0.2.17): arrayOf<Any?>(...). Status: UNCONFIRMED until the next CI run shows a clean androidtest-compile.log.
      Note: the main build stayed green because that step is non-blocking (this was the intended design).
      UPDATE v1.20: CONFIRMED. CI androidtest-compile.log on v0.2.17 reports BUILD SUCCESSFUL, 0 errors; compileDebugAndroidTestKotlin executed.


31.2 Database design problems

P-10  Room @Entity cannot declare SQL CHECK constraints, so the 48 canonical CHECK clauses were missing in the real database.
      Fix (v0.2.6): 46 BEFORE INSERT/UPDATE triggers (23 tables x 2) generated from the canonical SQL; NULL semantics match CHECK.
      Offline fuzz (about 124,000 operations): 0 differences. Counts 48 CHECK vs 46 triggers reconciled. Status: CONFIRMED at JDBC level;
      runtime on the real Room database UNCONFIRMED (RoomRuntimeTest not run yet).
      UPDATE v1.20: CONFIRMED on the real Room database (emulator API 34, v0.2.17): RoomRuntimeTest.checkEmulationRejectsInvalidRows passed (9 of 9).


P-11  Bengali full-text search: default tokenizer split words at vowel signs and hasanta.
      Fix (v0.2.6): unicode61 with tokenchars for Bengali vowel signs, hasanta, chandrabindu, visarga, anusvara, nukta; FTS update trigger fires on the right columns.
      Offline prototype: conjunct words and prefixes searchable. Status: CONFIRMED offline; device confirmation UNCONFIRMED.
      UPDATE v1.20: CONFIRMED on device for whole words and prefixes (banglaWordsAndPrefixesAreSearchable passed). Unicode spelling variants are F-2 (P-15).


P-12  Redundant indexes and uncovered foreign keys (scale risk, several hundred thousand questions).
      Fix (v0.2.14): removed 2 duplicate indexes; added 12 indexes so every foreign key is covered; Room entities and strict parity test match;
      WAL set explicitly. Status: CONFIRMED (run 18: 48 indices, 39 foreign keys, 7/7 tests). WAL actually active on device: UNCONFIRMED.
      UPDATE v1.20: WAL CONFIRMED on emulator (walJournalModeIsActive passed). Index coverage was confirmed earlier (run 18).


P-13  Schema drift risk between canonical SQL and Room entities.
      Fix (v0.2.13-0.2.14): RoomSchemaParityTest compares tables, columns, keys, foreign keys and indexes (strict). Status: CONFIRMED.

P-14  F-1 FTS delete/update scans the whole FTS table (question_id is an UNINDEXED FTS column).
      Evidence (offline prototype only): 18.3 ms per row at 50,000 rows vs 0.019 ms by rowid; cost grows linearly with row count.
      Status: OPEN. Plan: confirm with ScaleBenchmarkTest on the emulator, then decide ADR-003 (recommended: integer surrogate column used as FTS rowid;
      database is still version 1 and unreleased, so no migration is needed). Canonical schema is not changed before that decision.
      UPDATE v1.20: CONFIRMED by measurement at 300,000 rows (section 32). Fix in v0.2.18 is UNCONFIRMED until CI and the emulator run pass.
      UPDATE v1.21: FIX CONFIRMED (CI run 24 and two emulator runs; section 33). Edit 0.19-0.20 ms, delete 0.58-0.70 ms per row at 300,000 rows. Status: CLOSED.


P-15  F-2 Unicode normalization: precomposed and decomposed forms of the letter য় (and similar letters) do not match each other in search.
      Status: OPEN. Plan: normalize text on write and on query (NFC plus folding), Phase 12.
      UPDATE v1.20: STILL OPEN on v0.2.17 (decomposed query found 0 rows). Fix in v0.2.18 (NFC on write and query) is UNCONFIRMED.
      UPDATE v1.21: FIX CONFIRMED (RoomRuntimeTest.banglaNormalizationMakesBothSpellingsSearchable passed; benchmark log: precomposed=1 decomposed=1). Status: CLOSED for the DB layer. Remaining: every text entry point must call BanglaText.normalize (Phase 12 acceptance item).


31.3 Planning and documentation problems

P-16  Phase 1 was recorded as DONE although DI, navigation/UI foundation, release variant, instrumentation run and version-freeze document were open.
      Fix (v1.17/v1.18): status re-baselined to "build gate passed, foundation incomplete". Status: CONFIRMED (documented).

P-17  Plan header said version 1.9 while the content was 1.16; the ZIP carried a plan file with an old name; BUILD_STATUS.txt was stale.
      Fix: header and file name follow the version; BUILD_STATUS.txt is refreshed with every package. Status: CONFIRMED.

P-18  Phase 0 deliverables (146-item traceability sheet, screen inventory, requirements spec, risk register) not produced before Phase 2 work.
      Status: OPEN. Scheduled in parallel, kept light (section 29.2, step C).

31.4 Working rules learned (apply from now on)
1. A fix is marked CONFIRMED only after a CI run or test proves it; otherwise it is written as UNCONFIRMED.
2. A green build counts only if the key tasks really executed (no FROM-CACHE for ksp, compile, tests).
3. New code that has never been compiled is introduced together with a compile check in CI.
4. Failed attempts stay in the log with their result.
5. Every package refreshes BUILD_STATUS.txt, the version notes, and this log.
6. Scale decisions (several hundred thousand questions with solutions and images) are measured, not assumed.

31.5 Change log addition
- v1.19: Problem and solution log (P-01 to P-18) and working rules added.

END OF VERSION 1.19 UPDATE


============================================================
32. BENCHMARK RESULTS, ADR-003 AND PACKAGE v0.2.18 - VERSION 1.20
============================================================

Planning update date: 17 November 2026

32.1 Measured results (emulator sdk_gphone64_x86_64, API 34, GitHub Actions runner; package v0.2.17)
Both instrumented runs: Gradle exit code 0, 9 of 9 tests passed (8 runtime tests + 1 benchmark). Unit tests 7 of 7.

Metric                              20,000 rows    300,000 rows
Import total                        6.1 s          74.9 s (4,004 rows/s average, about 5,500 rows/s late)
Database size                       39 MB          589 MB (about 2 KB per question with 2 solution parts)
WAL peak before checkpoint          20.7 MB        38.8 MB
FTS optimize                        20 ms          755 ms
Search common word, LIMIT 50        0.7 ms         0.7 ms
Search common word, ORDER BY rank   7.3 ms         109.4 ms
Search prefix, LIMIT 50             0.8 ms         3.7 ms
Search + topic join, LIMIT 50       10.7 ms        25.8 ms
Topic first page                    0.8 ms         0.7 ms
Global deep OFFSET                  4.9 ms         275.2 ms (offset 150,000)
Edit per row (FTS trigger)          5.12 ms        127.72 ms
Delete per row (FTS trigger)        5.51 ms        117.73 ms
FTS delete by question_id           4.28 ms        105.22 ms
FTS delete by rowid                 0.044 ms       0.117 ms

Caveats: x86 emulator on a CI runner, synthetic Latin-and-Bengali text, 2 solution parts per question, no images.
Treat as a relative baseline, not as phone speed. dbstat was unavailable, so per-table sizes are unknown.
The earlier estimate of 70-80 ms per edit at 300,000 rows was too low; the measured cost grows faster than linearly.

32.2 Findings
- F-1 CONFIRMED: edit and delete cost 118-128 ms per row at 300,000 rows (about 900 times slower than rowid keying).
  Deleting 10,000 questions would take roughly 20 minutes. Not acceptable.
- F-2 STILL OPEN on v0.2.17: indexed precomposed U+09DF, decomposed query found 0 rows.
- NEW (design notes, not defects yet):
  N-1 ORDER BY rank on a common word costs 109 ms at 300,000 rows. Keep rank sorting optional or bounded in the UI.
  N-2 Deep OFFSET paging costs 275 ms at offset 150,000. Use Paging 3 with keyset (cursor) queries, not OFFSET.

32.3 ADR-003 decision (F-1): option (a), integer surrogate column used as the FTS rowid, app-assigned
- Column: fts_id INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE on type_cq_questions and chapter_cq_questions.
- Why app-assigned: a missing or duplicate value fails loudly (NOT NULL / UNIQUE) instead of corrupting search silently;
  FTS triggers stay simple; no extra UPDATE per insert; the DAO layer is not written yet, so a single central allocator
  (FtsIdAllocator, read MAX once per write transaction) can be enforced.
- Rejected: using the implicit rowid or setting fts_id = rowid in a trigger. Question tables have a TEXT primary key,
  so VACUUM may renumber rowids; a later insert could then collide with an old fts_id. A trigger using MAX(fts_id)+1
  would need a nullable column and an extra UPDATE per insert.
- Rejected: option (c) (indexed question_id column inside FTS) - depends on IDs tokenizing as one token.
- Rules: fts_id is internal; never written to backups or exports; restore and import allocate new values.
- FTS is derived data: it can be rebuilt from the question tables; a maintenance routine (row-count check and rebuild)
  belongs to Phase 12/Backup work.

32.4 F-2 decision
- NFC on write and on every query (BanglaText.normalize). U+09DF, U+09DC and U+09DD are Unicode composition
  exclusions, so NFC alone maps them to base letter + nukta (checked with Python unicodedata); no folding table.
- NFC also unifies split and composed vowel signs. Existing sources of text (editor, import, restore, search box)
  must all call BanglaText.normalize; this is a Phase 12 acceptance item.

32.5 Package v0.2.18 (written, NOT compiled, NOT run - UNCONFIRMED)
See VERSION_NOTES_0.2.18.md. Offline prototype (Python sqlite3 3.45, 100,000 rows): edit 0.040 ms and delete 0.044 ms per
row, FTS count equals question count, missing/duplicate fts_id rejected, NFC search finds both spellings. Not a device result.

32.6 Status
PHASE 1: BUILD GATE PASSED, FOUNDATION INCOMPLETE (unchanged).
PHASE 2: runtime verification on emulator DONE for v0.2.17 (CHECK emulation, FK, WAL, FTS sync, Bengali words/prefixes,
         scale baseline). F-1 and F-2 fixes written, UNCONFIRMED.
PHASES 3-17: NOT STARTED.

32.7 Problem log additions
P-19  N-1 rank search 109 ms at 300,000 rows. Status: OPEN (design note; decide in Phase 12).
P-20  N-2 deep OFFSET paging 275 ms at offset 150,000. Status: OPEN (use keyset paging in the DAO layer).

32.8 Next steps (in order)

Metric                          v0.2.17        v0.2.18 run A   v0.2.18 run B
Import total                    74.9 s         101.9 s         85.2 s
Import average rows/s           4,004          2,945           3,519
Database size                   589 MB         584,012 KB      584,012 KB
Edit per row                    127.72 ms      0.19 ms         0.20 ms
Delete per row                  117.73 ms      0.58 ms         0.70 ms

Consistency                     -              299,800 = 299,800  299,800 = 299,800
Caveats as in section 32.1 (x86 emulator on a shared CI runner). Run-to-run differences of 20-40 percent
(import speed, rank search, deep OFFSET) are normal noise here; only the large effects are conclusions.

33.3 Findings
- F-1 CONFIRMED FIXED: edit about 640 times faster, delete about 170-200 times faster. The target (well under 1 ms per row) is met.
  Deleting 10,000 questions now takes roughly 6-7 seconds instead of about 20 minutes. Delete is still 5-6 times slower than a bare
  FTS delete by rowid (0.117 ms) because it also cascades solution parts and fires the triggers.
- F-2 CONFIRMED FIXED for the database layer.
- Import is slower than v0.2.17 in both runs (2,945 and 3,519 vs 4,004 rows/s). Probable cause: the extra unique fts_id index and
  the larger FTS insert path. NOT proven; the runs also differ from each other by 20 percent. Import time of about 85-100 s for
  300,000 rows is acceptable for a one-time bulk import; no action now. Re-check if bulk import becomes a user-facing workflow.
- N-1 (rank search 146-171 ms) and N-2 (deep OFFSET 159-252 ms) remain open design notes (P-19, P-20): keep rank sorting
  optional or bounded, and use keyset paging.

33.4 ADR-002 decision (UI toolkit and DI) - ACCEPTED, proposed by the assistant, owner may overrule
- Jetpack Compose with Material 3, single Activity, Navigation Compose; Paging 3 with keyset queries.
- Manual constructor injection via AppContainer; no Hilt/Dagger/Koin for now. Reasons and consequences are in
  docs/Architecture_Decision_Records.md. Compose and Navigation are NOT yet in the build; they arrive in v0.2.20 with one screen
  and CI verification.

36. v0.2.21 EMULATOR RESULT, DEFECT F-3 AND PACKAGE v0.2.22 - VERSION 1.24
============================================================

Planning update date: 18 November 2026

36.1 Evidence (instrumented report, benchmarkRows=20000, emulator API 34, package v0.2.21)
- Gradle exit 1. The app, the three new DAOs, the repository and all androidTest sources compiled (Room accepted the INSERT ... SELECT queries;
  lifecycle-viewmodel-compose 2.10.0 resolved, P-28 effectively closed for compile).
- 10 RoomRuntimeTest tests and ScaleBenchmarkTest passed. 7 TypeCqHierarchyRepositoryTest tests failed. The result XML labels the suite
  RoomRuntimeTest with tests=18, failures=7: the 18 are 10 + 1 + 7, so the expected total in section 35.5 (19) was wrong; it is 18.
- 20,000-row baseline (closes P-22): edit 0.11 ms and delete 0.11 ms per row, common-word search 1.1 ms, rank-ordered search 9.9 ms,
  prefix 0.8 ms, topic join 6.3 ms, deep OFFSET at 10,000: 4.7 ms, FTS rows = question rows (19,800 = 19,800), checkpoint 5 ms, database 38.8 MB.

36.2 Finding F-3 (confirmed from logcat)
HierarchyName used Regex("(?U)\\s+"). Android's regex engine throws PatternSyntaxException for the (?U) flag, so the HierarchyName class could not load and
every add-subject/chapter/topic call failed. The JVM unit tests (HierarchyNameTest) passed because a desktop JDK accepts the flag; the normal CI therefore could not detect it.
Lesson (rule added): behaviour that depends on the Java/Android runtime (regex, Normalizer is fine, text, locale, file system) must have an
instrumented test; a green JVM test is not evidence for Android. The v0.2.21 APK would have crashed on the first add: it must not be used.

36.3 Package v0.2.22 (written, NOT compiled, NOT run - UNCONFIRMED)
- HierarchyName.clean as a plain loop over Char.isWhitespace(); same rules; documented in ADR-004.
- New HierarchyNameAndroidTest (2 emulator tests). No schema change.

36.4 Status
PHASE 4/5: hierarchy create/list implemented; repository behaviour still unconfirmed because its tests did not get past F-3.
Step D: in progress. Everything else as in section 35.3.

36.5 Problem log additions
F-3   Android regex flag crash in HierarchyName. Status: FIX WRITTEN (v0.2.22), unconfirmed.
P-22  CLOSED (20,000-row baseline recorded).
P-28  Compile part CLOSED; runtime part unconfirmed.
P-29  Process gap: the normal CI runs JVM tests only; Android-runtime defects appear only in the manual emulator workflow. Status: OPEN.
      Decision: run the emulator workflow (20,000 rows) before every phone install of a package that adds runtime logic. Consider a
      non-blocking emulator job on every push later.

36.6 Next steps (in order)
1. Push v0.2.22; send the normal CI report (expect 17 of 17, schema hash 786b471d...).

37. v0.2.22 PHONE TEST PASSED AND PACKAGE v0.2.23 (QUESTION EDITOR, NEVER LOSING A QUESTION) - VERSION 1.25
============================================================

Planning update date: 18 November 2026

37.1 Evidence (owner test of v0.2.22 on a phone)
- CI report OK. Instrumented test OK. Adding subject, chapter and topic OK. Two items with the same name are not created.
- F-3 (Android regex flag) is CLOSED. P-29 stays open (the normal CI still runs JVM tests only).
- First open of the app takes about 5 seconds. Owner decision: acceptable as long as later updates do not make it longer; even 10 seconds is
  acceptable. Recorded as requirement N-1 (cold start at most 10 s). Likely causes: debug APK, first Room open with FTS tables and triggers, Compose
  cold start. Possible later measures (low priority): release build with R8, baseline profile, opening the database off the main thread.
- Owner requirement for this step: the app must be reliable enough that questions added to it are never lost (requirement N-2).

37.2 Findings that shaped v0.2.23
- N-2 risk 1 (found by reading the workflow): CI builds the debug APK on a fresh runner, which creates a new random debug key each time.
  Android refuses to update an app signed with another key, so every update would need an uninstall, which deletes the database. FIXED by a committed
  fixed debug key (ADR-005 item 5). One last uninstall is needed when moving from v0.2.22.
- N-2 risk 2: there is no backup. Data lives in app-private storage; "allowBackup" is false. Uninstall, "Clear data" or a lost phone deletes everything.
  NOT fixed in v0.2.23; proposed as the next package (P-31).
- N-2 risk 3: text typed but not yet saved is lost if the app is killed. FIXED by automatic drafts (ADR-005 items 3).

37.3 Package v0.2.23 (written, NOT compiled, NOT run - UNCONFIRMED)
- Fixed debug signing key, versionCode 23, CI step that records the APK certificate (apk-signature.txt; SHA-256 must be identical in every run).
- Question types seeded for ক, খ, গ, ঘ (1, 2, 3, 4 marks; assumption, ids fixed, labels renamable).
- TypeCqQuestionDao (abstract class, @Transaction insertGroup), TypeCqQuestionRepository(+Impl), QuestionRules, draft storage in app_metadata.
- Editor screen (stimulus + four blocks), list screen per topic (newest first, grouped by stimulus), topic list now opens the question list.
- Durable commits: PRAGMA synchronous=FULL on the writer connection before each save (outside a transaction).
- All-or-nothing save with read-back verification; save is non-cancellable once started.
- Not included: edit, delete, search UI, boards, categories, images, solutions, Paging 3 (lists are read as one list per topic).
- No schema change: database version 1, identityHash must remain 786b471dfa14c9ae4739bb81479bab25.
- Tests: 11 new JVM tests (QuestionRulesTest), 10 new emulator tests (TypeCqQuestionRepositoryTest).

   a. Home -> ব্যাকআপ ও রিস্টোর -> ব্যাকআপ তৈরি করুন -> save the file in Drive or another place outside the phone; "ব্যাকআপ সম্পন্ন ও যাচাই করা হয়েছে" must appear.
   b. Add one more question; open the backup screen: it must warn that the question count changed.
   c. Restore from the file of step a: the confirmation must show the backup count (one less) and the current count; confirm; close the app when asked; reopen;
      the extra question must be gone, the older ones present, and the screen must say the restore is complete.
   d. Also try restoring a wrong file (for example a photo): it must be refused and nothing may change.
4. Then v0.2.25 (part B): question search, edit and delete.
5. Confirm or change the question-type labels and marks (P-32), still open.

38.6 Problem log additions
P-31  No backup/export of the database. Status: FIX WRITTEN (v0.2.24), unconfirmed.
P-34  Restore needs one app restart (the live database connection is never replaced). Status: ACCEPTED BY DESIGN (ADR-006), re-check wording on the phone.
P-35  Backup is manual; the owner must remember it (the screen warns when the question count changed). Status: OPEN, low priority; scheduled backup is a later option.
P-36  Backup file is not encrypted or password protected. Status: OPEN, low priority.
P-30  Fixed debug key. Status: confirmed by the owner's v0.2.23 run only for question saving; update-over-install test is step 3 above.

38.7 Change log addition
- v1.26: v0.2.23 phone test result, next step split into part A (v0.2.24 backup) and part B (v0.2.25 search/edit/delete), package v0.2.24, problems P-34 to P-36.

END OF VERSION 1.26 UPDATE

39. UI DESIGN BASELINE (caapbuild-updated-21), v0.2.24 TEST PROGRESS AND REVISED PACKAGE ORDER - VERSION 1.28
============================================================

Planning update date: 18 November 2026
(Version 1.27 had a shorter section 39 written after a partial reading. Version 1.28 replaces it after reading all screens.)

39.1 Evidence received since v1.26 (package v0.2.24)
1. CI run: SUCCESSFUL (owner report). Instrumented (emulator) test: SUCCESSFUL (owner report).
   The counts expected in 38.5 (35 unit tests, 37 emulator tests, identityHash, apk-signature SHA-256) were not sent to the
   assistant. Status: reported green by the owner; counts NOT re-verified by the assistant.

3. Not yet reported: install over v0.2.23 without uninstall (P-30), warning after the question count changes, restore from the
   backup file with confirmation and restart (P-34), refusal of a wrong file.
   Consequence: P-31 is PARTLY confirmed (export works). Restore, P-30 and P-34 stay UNCONFIRMED until the owner reports.

39.2 What caapbuild-updated-21 is, and how it was read
- A web-technology app: React 19, TypeScript, Tailwind CSS 4, Vite, Capacitor 7 (Android shell), Dexie 4 (IndexedDB), KaTeX,
  jsPDF + html2canvas-pro, react-zoom-pan-pinch, lucide-react, font Hind Siliguri. package.json: qb-collection-exam 1.1.0,
  appId com.qbcollection.examv2. About 33,000 lines in src/, 30 vitest test files, a GitHub Actions APK workflow.
- It is a DIFFERENT technology stack from this project (Kotlin / Room / Compose, ADR-001, ADR-002). RULE: it is a design and
  behaviour REFERENCE. No code is copied. Every screen is rebuilt in Jetpack Compose (Material 3) on top of the repositories (section 3.1).
- How it was read (honest scope): for every screen and component the assistant read the comments, all visible Bengali labels,
  state and handler names, and the key render blocks (question card, menus, form sections, PDF layout rules). It did NOT read every
  line of the 33,000 lines. Not read in detail: the folder-sync code (src/storage/, about 300 KB, not adopted, see D-1), the sync
  preview dialogs, the native Java (only the method list), package-lock.json, the bodies of the test files (names only), and the
  second half of pdfGenerator.ts (page factory / save). When a screen is built, its source file is re-read for that screen.
- The 146-item Feature-to-Schema Audit Matrix is referenced by this plan but was not among the uploaded files, so this section
  does not map design features to the 146 numbered items. That mapping stays an open Phase 0 task (39.13).

39.3 Design inventory - screens and behaviours (source: the reference app)
NAVIGATION
- One window, five bottom tabs in this order: Create | Type CQ | Chapter CQ | Filter | Profile. Active tab: blue icon in a light-blue pill.
- Android Back works as a priority stack: the topmost dialog or inner view closes first; at the root a confirmation asks before leaving.
- Last position inside Type CQ / Chapter CQ is remembered when switching tabs. The Filter tab can jump to "where this question is" and the
  card flashes there.
HEADER (every screen): blue bar, optional back arrow, centred title with optional small subtitle, optional search icon, 3-dot menu.
  Screen titles seen: "তৈরি (Create)" / "পরীক্ষা ও প্রশ্নপত্র ব্যবস্থাপনা"; "প্রোফাইল (Profile)" / "পরিসংখ্যান, ব্যাকআপ ও সেটিংস";
  "বিষয় তালিকা (Subjects)" and then the subject / chapter / topic name.

TYPE CQ and CHAPTER CQ TABS (same screen, two scopes)
- Levels: Type CQ = subjects -> chapters -> topics -> questions. Chapter CQ = subjects -> chapters -> questions (the reference hides a
  default topic; this plan has separate Chapter_CQ tables without topics, which fits).
- Under the header: a horizontally scrollable breadcrumb (each part tappable), a "Reorder / Done" button on list levels, and on the
  question level a "N নির্বাচিত" pill with "Clear all selection". Right side: counter "মোট বিষয়: N টি" / "মোট ... টি".
- List cards: blue rounded square with the 2-digit Bengali serial, name, line "N টি প্রশ্ন · N নির্বাচিত", chevron, 3-dot (নাম পরিবর্তন,
  মুছে ফেলুন). Reorder mode shows up/down arrows (শেষে ক্রম সংরক্ষণ). Empty states with a "+ ... যোগ করুন" button.
- Header menu on list levels: new subject/chapter/topic, rename and delete of the open item, Import / Export (subject level),
  clear all selection. Header menu on the question level: JSON paste import, select all / deselect, copy selected (N), cut selected (N),
  paste copied (N), delete selected (N).
- Search icon in the header (question level only): box "প্রশ্ন, উদ্দীপক বা বোর্ড খুঁজুন...". The reference does a plain lower-case substring
  match over stimulus text, board tag, question text, solution text (picture marker lines removed) and board names and years (Latin and
  Bengali digits). A card shows only the sub-questions that matched; a match in the stimulus shows all.
- Floating "+" button adds a question to the open topic.
- Shared clipboard that works between Type CQ and Chapter CQ (copy / cut of a stimulus group or many selected questions; paste before,
  after, or at the end). A banner shows "N টি প্রশ্ন কপি/কাট করা আছে" with a clear button.
QUESTION CARD (one card per stimulus group)
- Blue ribbon: "প্রশ্ন N" (running number), a checkbox selecting all sub-questions of the stimulus, a yellow rating badge (stars text such as
  "৩★"), a 3-dot menu: Edit this Q&A, রেফারেন্স দেখুন, Copy, Cut, Paste after, Paste before, Delete.
- Light-grey chips row: paper-usage chips (emerald, "<paper short name> ×N") and board chips written like DB'25,26 (stimulus boards only;
  each sub-question's own boards sit beside its own button); "ট্যাগ নেই" when none.
- Body: stimulus pictures placed above the text (imageTopCount) then text then the remaining pictures; sub-questions "গ." "ঘ." with text;
  per question a small "সমাধান দেখো / সমাধান লুকাও" button; the solution panel "সমাধান:" shows ordered text boxes and pictures;
  tapping a picture opens a zoom viewer. Math is shown rendered (KaTeX); a wrong formula shows the faulty code in red, never crashes.
- Rating on the card = the stimulus rating (rule U-1).

QUESTION EDITOR (full-screen form, title "প্রশ্ন যোগ করো" / "প্রশ্ন সম্পাদনা", "অসংরক্ষিত" tag, undo, redo, save)
- Mode switch "Minimum | Full Editor". Minimum is ALWAYS the default each time the form opens.
- Minimum: stimulus box (2 rows) with board picker, rating, add-picture "+" at start; reference box; per question block: type label, marks
  label, board picker, rating, eye (live preview), question box (2 rows), solution = ordered boxes (text box, picture "+", new box "+"),
  save. Topic is taken from where the form was opened.
- Full adds: collapsible subject / chapter / topic card (topic required), math toolbar (inline $, block $$, fraction, root, power, subscript,
  sum, integral, 16 Greek letters), larger boxes with a drag handle, type chooser and editable marks for each question, delete-block button,
  "ক-খ-গ-ঘ সাজাও" (sort blocks), "+ আরও প্রশ্ন যোগ করো", short answer box "সংক্ষিপ্ত উত্তর (ঐচ্ছিক)" with preview, picture tools (replace,
  export, big zoom preview).
- Both modes: board dropdown opens a year window (tap years, or "অন্য সাল লিখুন"); chips with remove; custom boards ("নতুন বোর্ড যুক্ত করুন"
  code + name); rating 1-5 (সহজ, মোটামুটি, মাঝারি, ভালো, চ্যালেঞ্জিং; default 3); undo/redo with grouped typing; marks follow the type
  while still at the default (ক 1, খ 2, গ 3, ঘ 4); validation errors; delete-block confirmation; "এই প্রশ্ন আগে থেকেই আছে" duplicate warning with
  "তবু সংরক্ষণ করো / ফিরে যাও" (never silently blocked; compares text ignoring spaces, $ marks, brackets and punctuation, in BOTH Type CQ and
  Chapter CQ); unsaved-changes prompt on close; Back closes inner windows first. Mode switching keeps all values.
- Pictures: added from the phone, optional editor (rotate left/right, crop, scan mode gray / black-and-white, can be switched off in Settings),
  stored as WebP, longest side 800 px, quality 0.8; export as PNG to Download/QbCollection/Images.

FILTER TAB
- Search box "প্রশ্ন, উদ্দীপক বা সমাধান খুঁজুন..." (debounced) and a row of filter chips that can be re-ordered by long-press-drag (personal layout).
- Filter window with sections: উৎস (সব / Type CQ / Chapter CQ), সাজান (default, rating high-low, rating low-high, marks high-low, type),
  বিষয়, অধ্যায়, টাইপ / টপিক (Type CQ only), প্রশ্নের ধরন (ক-ঘ), রেটিং, নম্বর, বোর্ড, সাল, যে ক্যাটেগরিতে ব্যবহৃত হয়েছে,
  বোর্ডে এসেছে কিনা (সব / এসেছে / আসেনি), উদ্দীপক (সব / আছে / নেই), সমাধান (সব / আছে / নেই).
  Hidden choices drop out when their parent changes (no invisible filters).
- Result cards are grouped by stimulus and show only the matching sub-questions; same checkbox selection as the Collection tabs.
- Saved filters: name the current filter + sort, apply with one tap, delete. Filters survive tab switches.
- Header menu: save this filter, saved filters (N), select all (N), invert, clear selection, PDF বানান, রেটিং বদলান (bulk), কপি, কাট, মুছে ফেলুন
  (confirmation: "সমাধানের ছবিসহ স্থায়ীভাবে মুছে যাবে").

CREATE TAB (question papers)
- List of papers with category filter chips and search "নাম বা শিরোনাম দিয়ে খুঁজুন"; each paper shows a short name = category name + number
  (for example "উইকলি মডেল টেস্ট-১"), date, time, marks, and a "some questions not in the bank" badge when needed.
- Multi-select mode with bottom bar: ক্যাটেগরি (move), কপি, কাট, ডিলিট; paste; batch PDF export; floating "নতুন প্রশ্নপত্র".
- New paper = the questions SELECTED by checkboxes in the Type CQ / Chapter CQ / Filter tabs (one persistent selection shared by all three).
- Paper form: the included stimulus groups as cards, move up / down, remove from list, edit the question inside this paper only (the bank is
  never changed; marker "প্রশ্ন পরিবর্তিত"; can return to the original), warning chip when the same question is already in other papers,
  warning (never a block) when the question marks do not add up to the total marks, button to put a question that is missing from the bank back
  into the bank, unsaved-changes prompt. Paper information dialog: title *, category * (or "+ নতুন ক্যাটেগরি"), subject line, দ্রষ্টব্য note (blank =
  none, default from Settings), time, total marks.
- Paper view: student copy is the default; teacher copies "short answers" or "full solutions"; PDF text size control (remembered); edit paper; delete;
  download PDF.

PDF (this is the visual standard for the owner's papers)
- A4 portrait, white page always (also when the app is in dark mode). Margins about 34 px sides, 32 px top and bottom; two columns of 350 px with a 24 px
  divider; footer with page number.
- Page 1 header over both columns: title, subject line, time and total marks row, দ্রষ্টব্য note. Later pages: a small running header.
- Column 1 fills first, then column 2, then the next page. A question group is never cut across columns or pages (a group taller than a column is
  placed alone in an empty column so progress continues). End marker "— সমাপ্ত —".
- Group layout: running number beside the stimulus text, stimulus pictures above/below, sub-questions "গ." with the marks on the right, formulas on the
  same baseline as the text; teacher copy adds an answer or solution box and a suffix in the title and file name.
- Batch export: one new folder per category, file name = paper name. Filter tab can build one PDF from selected rows of both collections.
- The reference rasterises pages with html2canvas (large files, text not selectable). The owner's checklist test-data/PDF_TEST_CHECKLIST.md
  (3 stimuli, 11 questions, 4 pictures, long stimulus, 18-step solution, missing answers, dark-mode check) is kept as the acceptance test for D-7.

PROFILE TAB (the hub for categories, statistics, data and settings)
- Categories: list with paper counts, new, rename, copy and paste (duplicates the category), sort (own order, name, newest, oldest, most/fewest papers),
  re-order with arrows, multi-select (backup file, PDF export, delete). "অশ্রেণিবদ্ধ" is always pinned on top. Deleting a category moves its papers there.
  Inside a category: papers with sort / reorder / multi-select, copy, cut, paste, move, copy to another category, share as file, open in Create, PDF.
- পরিসংখ্যান: chips সব / Type CQ / Chapter CQ; total, used, unused; by board with years; ratings bars; question types; "weak part" = a chapter with fewer than half
  of the average number of questions. Computed only when the screen opens.
- Import / export: Type CQ file, Chapter CQ file (JSON v1.1, pictures embedded, preview with counts, merge or replace with a strong warning, manual picture
  attach, sample file), full backup (everything including papers, pictures, custom boards, filter layout), restore as replace or merge; category / paper share files.
- Settings: theme Light / Dark (applies to the app, not the PDF), text size by number 12-28 (default 16), default paper title, default দ্রষ্টব্য note,
  picture editor on/off, daily auto backup and "এখনই ব্যাকআপ নিন" with the result shown under the button.
- Auto backup: once a day, new folder named date_time with three files (Type CQ, Chapter CQ, full backup); checked at app start, on return and every 30 minutes.
- Share app, app info (version, build, date).
- JSON paste import (Type CQ and Chapter CQ question level menu): the owner photographs questions, an AI tool converts them to JSON using the prompt built into the
  app ("AI-এর জন্য prompt কপি"), and the owner pastes it. Format: groups[] with stimulus, stimulusRating, reference, stimulusBoards[], questions[] each with
  type, marks, rating, questionText, solutionText, answerText, usedInBoards[]. The parser is forgiving: removes ```json fences and text outside the JSON,
  trailing commas, raw line breaks inside strings, repairs single backslashes of LaTeX (\frac, \times, \theta ... so JSON does not turn \t into a TAB),
  accepts Bengali digits, guesses type from marks (1 ক, 2 খ, 3 গ, 4 ঘ) or marks from type, clamps rating to 1-5, accepts flat question arrays. Buttons: পেস্ট, নমুনা কপি.
  This is the owner's main data-entry route (REQUIREMENT U-3).

39.4 Design tokens to carry into Compose (visual parity is the target)
Light: primary #3B4FD8, primary dark #2A3BB0, brand light #EEF2FF, screen background #F2F4F8, card #FFFFFF, border #D5D9E2, text #1B1F2A, text blue #2A3BB0,
  muted #64748B, faint #94A3B8, chip row background #F8FAFC with border #E2E8F0. Rating badge: background #FEF9C3, text #713F12, border #FDE047.
  Paper-usage chip: emerald (background emerald-50, text emerald-800, border emerald-200). Delete: red. Cut: amber.
Dark: background #0F172A, card #1E293B, border #334155, text #F1F5F9, muted #94A3B8, primary text #818CF8 / #A5B4FC, selected chip #1E1B4B,
  rating badge dark #422006 with text #FDE68A, success #052E16, error #450A0A.
Typography: Hind Siliguri 400 / 500 / 600 for Bengali and Latin. Card text about 12 sp, header title 18 sp, chips 10 sp, nav labels 11 sp; everything scales
  from the Settings base size. Numbers on screen are written in Bengali digits (requirement U-2) with 2-digit padded serials (০১, ০২).
Shape and size: cards radius 16 dp, border 1 dp (question card 1.5 dp blue), touch targets at least 44 dp, header 56 dp, bottom nav 64 dp, FAB bottom right.
Theme control: owner-chosen Light / Dark in Settings, not the system theme. The current Compose theme follows the system and uses #315EFB with no custom font (P-37).
Status bar: blue with light icons, content not under it; Back at root asks before exit.
Reference helpers worth keeping as behaviour: safe-area insets, long-press-drag reorder of filter chips, remembered scroll / position per tab.

39.5 Data model comparison (to be VERIFIED against the 35 canonical tables in the traceability step)
- Entities in the design: Subject, Chapter, Topic, Stimulus (text, imageIds, imageTopCount, boardTag, usedInBoards[], rating, reference), Question (stimulusId, topicId,
  type, marks, questionText, solutionText + solutionImageIds, answerText, rating, order, usedInBoards[]), Category, Paper (shortName, category, title, subjectLine, note,
  duration, totalMarks, questionIds[], edits, order), DbImage (fileName, topicId, w, h), QuestionCopy (copy of a deleted-from-bank question kept for papers).
  The canonical schema already has counterparts for most of these (stimulus boards, question boards, solution parts, images, categories and links, papers, paper items,
  paper overrides, paper images). Fields to CHECK in the canonical SQL: stimulus rating, stimulus reference, image top count, answer text, question order, paper order,
  paper note / duration / total marks, custom boards.
- Solution: the design keeps ONE text with marker lines [[img:<id>]] and [[box]]. This plan uses the ordered solution-parts table. Do not copy the marker trick.
  The importer (D-2) converts markers to ordered parts.
- Rating rule U-1 (from CHANGES.txt, applies to cards, filter, sort, statistics): stimulus rating if saved; else the question's own rating (a card with several questions
  shows the average); no stimulus: the question's rating, else 3.
- Paper-only edits map to paper item overrides. A deleted-from-bank question still shown in a paper maps to the immutable paper snapshot (sections 3.5, 13); the plan keeps
  the snapshot as the authority and adds the design's "put it back into the bank" action (U-6).
- Persistent selection (checkbox on cards, shared by Type CQ, Chapter CQ, Filter and Create): the design keeps it in the database. The canonical schema has no obvious
  table for it. Needs a decision (D-10): app_metadata, or a new table through a migration.
- Things the design keeps in the phone's localStorage and that MUST be in the database here so the backup contains them: custom boards, saved filters, filter chip order,
  settings, last PDF text size, remembered sort modes.
- Scale: the reference loads whole lists into memory (filter rows with a search string for every question, statistics counted in memory, plain substring search). That
  cannot serve hundreds of thousands of questions. Same behaviour, different implementation: SQL queries with indexes and FTS, Paging 3, SQL aggregates (R-U3).

 Not adopted: folder sync (README "Folder sync"), MANAGE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE permission, WebView/Capacitor shell.

39.7 Where the design and this plan disagree (the plan stays the authority until the owner decides)
- Folders as master vs one Room database as master (ADR-001, ADR-006). Not adopted (D-1).
- Reference papers point to live questions plus copies; this plan requires immutable snapshots. Plan wins.
- Reference storage permission "All files access" and visible folders Download/QB Collection; this plan uses the Storage Access Framework with no broad permission.
- Reference has no transactional / crash-safety guarantees; the plan rules (atomic save, drafts, read-back, backup verification) stay.
- Reference shows one unsaved-changes prompt; this app keeps automatic drafts (ADR-005) and adds the prompt only where useful.
- Reference deletion removes pictures permanently without any backup reminder; the plan requires the delete-safety design first.

39.8 Decisions needed from the owner (recommendation in brackets; nothing below is decided yet)
 D-1  Folder sync with visible folders: adopt or not? [NO. Database + backup file (done) + later an optional readable export.]
 D-2  Must the new app read data made in the reference app? [YES if the owner has real data there. Build an importer for its Type CQ / Chapter CQ JSON v1.1 files and
      full backup (pictures are embedded as base64), NOT for its folder format. Decide before Phase 10 so the schema keeps what the files carry.]
 D-3  Theme: owner-chosen Light / Dark and text size in Settings, not the system theme. [YES]
 D-4  Brand colour #3B4FD8 instead of #315EFB. [YES]
 D-5  JSON paste import: include? [YES, and EARLY: it is the owner's main way to enter many questions (U-3). Needs the stimulus rating, reference, boards, answer text
      fields first (UI-07 to UI-09). Ask the owner for one real AI-made JSON sample to test the forgiving parser.]
 D-6  Mathematics in Compose: (a) WebView + bundled KaTeX per card, (b) native Compose LaTeX library, (c) formula rendered once to an image and cached.
      [Prototype all feasible options with the owner's PDF sample and measure on a real phone; record ADR-007 before UI-15.]
 D-7  PDF method: Android PdfDocument with own two-column layout, or WebView print. Acceptance = the reference layout of 39.3 and the checklist in test-data.
      [Prototype first; record ADR-008. Prefer selectable text and small files over the reference's rasterised pages.]
 D-8  P-32 types and marks: the reference uses ক 1, খ 2, গ 3, ঘ 4 with editable marks and the AI prompt uses the same. [Close P-32 as confirmed.]
 D-9  Rating rule U-1 into requirements. [YES]
 D-10 Where to keep the persistent question selection. [app_metadata for now; a table only if the Create tab needs per-paper history.]
 D-11 Bengali digits everywhere in the UI through one formatter. [YES]
 D-12 Search: the plan uses FTS; the reference uses plain substring. [FTS for text; board and year search through normal indexed columns, accepting Bengali and Latin
      digits. Behaviour to reproduce: stimulus, question, solution text, boards, years.]
 D-13 Picture export location: reference writes Download/QbCollection/Images. [Use the Storage Access Framework or MediaStore; no broad storage permission.]
 D-14 Automatic daily backup: [Add after restore is confirmed; WorkManager once a day; keeps N latest files; decide N.]

39.9 Revised package order (recommendation; supersedes 38.2 / 38.5 steps 4-5 only after the owner agrees)
 v0.2.24  FINISH: owner reports the remaining phone steps. Nothing new is built.
 v0.2.25  UI SHELL (no schema change): Light/Dark setting, #3B4FD8 palette, Hind Siliguri font resource, text-size setting, Bengali digit formatter, five-tab bottom
          navigation with real screens for Type CQ and Profile (backup), placeholders for the others, blue header component, existing screens moved in.
          Checks: cold start still at most 10 s (N-1), identityHash 786b471dfa14c9ae4739bb81479bab25 unchanged.

          (UI-06 to UI-10). Needs a schema check (39.5) first; a migration only if a field is missing.

          PDF (UI-19 to UI-23, after the D-7 prototype) -> Chapter CQ (UI-27) -> settings, auto backup, regression, release.
 Rule kept: every package is CI-verified, emulator-verified and phone-verified before the next one starts (section 31.4).
 Tests to port as Kotlin unit tests from the reference's logic tests: rating rule U-1, filter semantics, duplicate-text normalisation, solution-part parsing, paper-only edits and
 mark totals, JSON paste parser, Bengali digit conversion.

39.10 New requirements
 U-1  Rating rule (39.5).   U-2  Bengali digits in all displayed numbers.   U-3  JSON paste import is a core data-entry route.
 U-4  The PDF page is always white and black, independent of the app theme.
 U-5  Paper short name = category name + running number; "অশ্রেণিবদ্ধ" category always first; deleting a category moves its papers there.
 U-6  A question missing from the bank can be put back from a paper snapshot.
 U-7  Selection is persistent and shared by Type CQ, Chapter CQ, Filter and Create.
 U-8  Filters never hide silently: a choice that is no longer offered is dropped.

39.11 New risks
 R-U1  Mathematics rendering and editing in Compose (the reference relies on KaTeX). Mitigation: D-6 prototype.
 R-U2  PDF quality and two-column pagination with Bengali and formulas. Mitigation: D-7 prototype with test-data/pdf-test-collection.json and its checklist.
 R-U3  The reference does filtering, statistics and search in memory; this must be SQL + FTS + Paging 3 for hundreds of thousands of questions. Mitigation: build each
       screen on queries, measure with the 300,000-row benchmark data.
 R-U4  Scope growth: the design is much larger than the current plan. Mitigation: package order 39.9, UI-xx traceability, one package at a time.
 R-U5  Custom font, theme and card rendering may lengthen cold start (N-1). Mitigation: measure in v0.2.25.
 R-U6  Cards with rendered math and pictures in long lists can scroll slowly. Mitigation: lazy images, cached formula renders, measure on the phone.

42.1 Owner decision: the next step is split in two parts
The step of section 41.5 item 2 ("question card, Bengali FTS search in the header, open an existing question in the editor; UI-04, UI-05, part of UI-11") is split.
- PART A = v0.2.27: question card in the new design and Bengali search inside a topic. READ-ONLY: no write path is added, so this package cannot damage saved data. Written in this update.
- PART B = v0.2.28: open an existing question in the editor and save the change (first change of saved questions). Designed in 42.4, NOT written. Starts only after part A is confirmed
  (CI report + emulator report + phone check list), as in section 31.4 and as in the split of section 38.2.
Reason for the cut: A and B have different risk. A only reads. B is the first update of stored questions: FTS update trigger, a separate draft for edits, rules for what an edit may not do.

42.2 Package v0.2.27 (written, NOT compiled, NOT run - UNCONFIRMED; ADR-011)
- Card (UI-04, part): blue ribbon "প্রশ্ন N", yellow rating badge (rule U-1: stimulus rating, else average of the question ratings with ties rounded up, else 3), light chip strip ("ট্যাগ নেই" until boards exist), stimulus, sub-questions with label, type, marks, short answer. Light and dark colours from 39.4.
- Search (UI-05): 🔍 in the header of the question list opens a search box. Question text and short answer through FTS5 (fts_id join, ADR-003); the LAST word is a prefix, earlier words are whole words, all words must match;
  every word is sent as a quoted phrase, so FTS syntax characters and the words AND / OR / NEAR cannot break the query. Stimulus text by plain match of every word (the stimulus has no FTS table).
  A card shows only the sub-questions that matched; a stimulus match shows all of them. Search starts 300 ms after the last key. Text is NFC-normalised (finding F-2).
  Lines: "N টি প্রশ্ন পাওয়া গেছে", "কোনো প্রশ্ন পাওয়া যায়নি।", "খোঁজার জন্য অক্ষর বা সংখ্যা লিখুন।". Back closes the search first.
- Code: domain/question/QuestionSearchRules.kt, QuestionCardRules.kt; TypeCqQuestionRepository.search(); data/local/TypeCqFtsSearch.kt (reader connection, plain prepared statement, because Room cannot check FTS tables at compile time);
  DAO: rating columns added to the row query, selectByTopic, stimuliOfTopic; ui/question: QuestionCard, QuestionListLogic, QuestionListViewModel (search state), QuestionListScreen (search header).
- versionCode 27, versionName 0.2.27-question-card-search. NO schema change (database version 1, identityHash 786b471dfa14c9ae4739bb81479bab25 must stay). No new dependency.
- Tests: 17 new unit tests (QuestionSearchRules 10, QuestionCardRules 4, QuestionListLogic 3) -> expected 74 of 74. 8 new emulator tests (QuestionSearchTest) -> expected 59.
  The FTS expectations of the new tests were checked against a real SQLite 3.45.1 FTS5 table with the same tokenizer; the Kotlin code itself was not compiled or run in this environment (no Gradle).
- Not included: card checkbox and 3-dot menu, solution toggle and pictures, board / paper chips, highlighting of matches, search in solutions / boards / years, search across Bengali and Latin digits (P-46).

42.3 Evidence
None yet for v0.2.27. Everything above is UNCONFIRMED until the CI report, the emulator report and the phone check list (8 steps in VERSION_NOTES_0.2.27.md) have been received and read.

42.4 Part B (v0.2.28) - design for the owner to overrule before it is written
1. Entry: the card gets a 3-dot menu with one item, "সম্পাদনা" (the other items of the design come with copy / delete in v0.2.30). A group is identified by its stimulus id, or by the question id when it has no stimulus.
2. The editor opens with the stored texts (stimulus, four question texts, four short answers), title "প্রশ্ন সম্পাদনা". A group WITHOUT stimulus is one question: only its own block is editable.
3. Save is ONE transaction (updateGroup): stimulus text, question text and short answer of existing sub-questions (the FTS update trigger replaces the FTS row through fts_id), new rows for labels that were empty (new fts_id, next sort_order), updated_at. Read-back after commit, as for a new save. Marks and types stay fixed (editable marks come with the editor fields).
4. Refused with a message, nothing written: blanking the text of an existing sub-question (that is a delete, v0.2.30); blanking the stimulus of a group that has one; the group disappeared meanwhile. Same cleaning and limits as a new save (NFC, trim, 20,000 characters).
5. Edit drafts: separate key draft:type_cq:edit:<id> (the draft of a NEW question in the same topic is not touched); written automatically like the new-question draft, removed in the same transaction as the update, restored when the edit is opened again.
6. After saving: back to the list, snackbar "পরিবর্তন সংরক্ষিত হয়েছে ✓"; the card and the search result show the new text at once.
7. Tests planned: JVM for the pure "what changed / what is refused" rules; emulator for old word gone from FTS and new word found, rollback keeps the old text, blanking refused, missing label added, stimulus changed, draft lifecycle, other topic untouched.
8. No schema change expected (identityHash must stay).

44. v0.2.28: CI RUN 39 FAILED (COMPILE ERROR), ONE-LINE FIX WRITTEN - VERSION 1.35
============================================================

Planning update date: 18 November 2026

44.1 Evidence (QuestionBank-ci-report of run 39, read by the assistant)
- Run 39, run ID 44274729177, commit 645d4d0a, branch main, Java 17.0.21. Build step outcome: failure. Gradle exit code 1. BUILD FAILED in 50 s.
- The first failing task is :app:compileDebugKotlin. Two errors, both "Platform declaration clash" (JVM signature setStimulus(Ljava/lang/String;)V), in QuestionEditViewModel.kt: line 43 (`var stimulus by mutableStateOf("")` with `private set`) and line 101 (`fun setStimulus(value: String)`). The private setter of the property and the function have the same JVM signature.
- The release assemble and the androidTest compile fail with the same two errors. No APK exists, so apk-signature.txt only shows "app-debug.apk (No such file or directory)"; this is a consequence, not a second defect.
- Tasks before the failure ran normally: kspDebugKotlin finished and the exported schema 1.json has identityHash 786b471dfa14c9ae4739bb81479bab25 (unchanged, as expected for a package without schema change).
- No unit test ran (the build stopped at the compile step). Run 39 therefore says NOTHING yet about the 15 new tests, about the edit transaction or about the rules.

44.2 Cause and fix
- Cause: the package was written without a compiler (43.3 said NOT compiled). The same kind of defect as run 37 (43.1: QuestionListViewModel `query` and `setQuery`), now in the new edit screen.
- Fix (one rename, no behaviour change): in QuestionEditViewModel.kt `fun setStimulus(value: String)` is renamed `fun changeStimulus(value: String)`. The only caller is QuestionEditScreen.kt (the stimulus text field: `onValueChange = viewModel::changeStimulus`).
- QuestionEditorViewModel (the new-question editor) also has a function `setStimulus`, but it has no property named `stimulus` (the text lives in `draft`), so there is no clash; it is not changed. Its `setText` and `setAnswer` take an index plus a value (two parameters), so they cannot clash either.
- The assistant scanned the whole main source (app/src/main) for every `var x` that has a one-parameter `fun setX(...)` in the same file: QuestionEditViewModel.stimulus was the only match. The scan is a text search, not a compiler: the next CI run is the proof.
- VERSION_NOTES_0.2.28.md has a short "CI fix (run 39)" note. versionCode 28 and versionName 0.2.28-edit-question are unchanged: the package keeps its number, as v0.2.27 did after its fix.

44.3 Naming rule from now on (assistant decision, owner may overrule)
- N-1: a class that has a property `x` with a setter (also `private set`, also a Compose `by mutableStateOf` property) never gets a function `setX(value)`. Change functions are named `onXChange` or `changeX`. Reason: two defects of the same kind in two packages in a row (runs 37 and 39).

UNCONFIRMED. Written, fix for run 39 written, NOT yet compiled, NOT run. Everything in 43.3 still applies (89 unit tests expected, 68 emulator tests expected, 10-step phone check list). Section 43 stays valid except that the CI result below replaces "NOT compiled" once the next report arrives.

45. v0.2.28 CONFIRMED (OWNER REPORT); HASHTAG SEARCH FINDING - VERSION 1.36
============================================================

Planning update date: 18 November 2026

45.1 Evidence for v0.2.28 (owner report, 18 November 2026)
- CI run after the run 39 fix: clean. Instrumented (emulator) test: clean. Phone check list (10 steps of VERSION_NOTES_0.2.28.md): clean.
- The assistant did not receive the new CI and emulator reports in this round, so the counts (89 unit tests, 68 emulator tests, identityHash, debug key) are NOT read by the assistant; they are recorded as owner-reported clean.
RESULT: v0.2.28 CONFIRMED by owner report (UI-04 3-dot menu, UI-11 edit core: open an existing question, edit the stored texts, one all-or-nothing save). P-52 (run 39 compile clash) CLOSED: the fix compiled in the following CI run.

45.2 Finding from the phone check: a question with a hashtag is not shown by search
- Owner report: a hashtag was written in one question; searching for the hashtag does not show that question. The exact text typed in the search field was not stated. Everything else in the check list was correct; the question itself is saved and shown, so this is a search behaviour, not a data fault.
- Assistant check (read of QuestionSearchRules.kt and TypeCqQuestionRepositoryImpl.search, plus a test with SQLite 3.45 FTS5 using the same tokenizer string as the app; NOT run on the phone):
  1. "#" is not a letter or digit, so the FTS5 tokenizer (unicode61) treats it as a separator and never stores it.
  2. A search word without any letter or digit is dropped (rule of v0.2.27, section 42). Searching only "#" therefore sends nothing to the database: SearchOutcome.NoTerms.
  3. A search such as "#আলো", "#Optics", "#physics_cq" or "#২০২৪" DID find the tagged question in the test. The "#" is ignored and the word is matched, so the same word without "#" is found too: a hashtag cannot be told apart from the plain word.
- Conclusion: searching a hashtag by its text works, but the "#" sign itself can never be searched, so "show me all questions with a hashtag" is not possible. If the owner typed more than "#" and still saw nothing, this explanation is not enough and a new report with the exact text is needed.

45.3 Options (owner decides; nothing written yet)
- A) Hash-aware search: a search word that starts with "#" (also "#" alone) is matched as plain text, with the "#" character, in the question text, the short answer and the stimulus (Kotlin side, like the stimulus today; the repository already loads every group of the topic). Other words stay FTS. No schema change, read-only. Needs a few new unit tests in QuestionSearchRulesTest and one emulator test.
- B) Leave as is: the planned categories and the Filter tab (Phase 12, plan section 39) are the real tag system, and the hashtag stays an ordinary word in the text.
- Assistant recommendation: A, inside the next package (v0.2.29), because it is small and read-only. The owner may overrule.

45.4 Revised package order
No change from 43.4: v0.2.28 (CONFIRMED) -> v0.2.29 (B-2 edit drafts; plus option A if chosen) -> v0.2.30 editor fields -> v0.2.31 delete + selection -> v0.2.32 JSON paste import -> the rest as in 43.4.

============================================================
46. PACKAGE v0.2.29 (B-2 EDIT DRAFTS + HASHTAG SEARCH) WRITTEN - VERSION 1.37
============================================================

Planning update date: 18 November 2026

46.1 Basis
- v0.2.28 is CONFIRMED by owner report (section 45.1). The owner asked the assistant to do the next step and, if it is big, to do it step by step. The next step is v0.2.29 (45.4, 45.8 item 2).
- The owner did not answer the A/B question of 45.3. DECISION by the assistant (owner may overrule): option A (hash-aware search) is included, as recommended in 45.3, because it is small, read-only and needs no schema change. If the owner prefers B, remove the hashtag part (QuestionSearchRules, the search() change, 6 unit tests, 3 emulator tests); the drafts part is independent.
- The package was written in four steps: (1) data layer for edit drafts, (2) edit ViewModel / screen / navigation, (3) hashtag search + tests, (4) tests, version, notes, plan.

46.2 Part 1 - edit drafts (B-2), design as announced in 43.2
- Key `draft:type_cq:edit:<groupKey>` in app_metadata (groupKey = "s-<stimulusId>" or "q-<questionId>"). The value reuses the DraftText JSON of the new-question draft (stimulus, four texts, four short answers).
- Repository: loadEditDraft, saveEditDraft (a blank text is stored too, because emptying every block is a real edit state), discardEditDraft. Failures are swallowed on purpose (a draft must never break typing).
- DAO updateGroup got a draftKey parameter and deletes the draft as its last statement, in the SAME @Transaction (all or nothing, like insertGroup). A refused update (BlankedQuestion, BlankedStimulus, MissingQuestionText, TooLong) or NoChange writes nothing and keeps the draft.
- QuestionEditViewModel: every change is queued (conflated channel) to a writer in the application scope; the queued value is the typed text while it differs from the stored text, otherwise "remove the draft". On open, a stored draft is put on top of the stored text (only the blocks this screen can edit); a draft equal to the stored text is removed; a draft of a card that no longer exists is removed. Save stops the writer first, then updateGroup; on refusal or failure the writer restarts and the typed text is written as a draft again. discard() stops the writer, removes the draft, then leaves. After a successful save or a discard no draft is written any more (flag).
- Screen: status line (waiting / saved), blue line "আগের অসমাপ্ত সম্পাদনা ফিরিয়ে আনা হয়েছে।", discard dialog text says the draft is thrown away too. No visual change otherwise.
- Naming rule N-1 (44.3) checked: no function `setX` next to a property `x` was added (new members: restoredFromDraft, draftStatus, discard, finished).
- The saved question rows are never written by a draft: P-50 (an unsaved edit is lost when the app is killed) is solved by design; status UNCONFIRMED until the phone check.

46.3 Part 2 - hashtag search (option A of 45.3)
- tokens(): a word that starts with "#" is kept even when it has no letter or digit. ftsQuery(): hash words are left out of the FTS5 string (the last remaining word stays the prefix); only hash words -> null. hashWordsMatch(): every hash word must occur as plain text (case-insensitive for Latin letters) in question text + short answer.
- Repository search(): NoTerms only when there is no word at all. With only hash words nothing is asked from FTS5 and every question of the topic is checked by hashWordsMatch. With both kinds, the FTS5 ids and the hash rule must both hold. The stimulus keeps its plain match of ALL words (hash words included), so a stimulus match still shows the whole card.
- Result: "#" alone finds every question with a "#"; "#আলো" finds "#আলো" and "#আলোর" but not the bare word; the bare word "আলো" still finds tagged and untagged texts (unchanged). A hash inside a word (a#b) is not a hash word.
- Closes the finding of 45.2 / P-53 in code (UNCONFIRMED until the phone check, steps 9-10). Open point: the exact text the owner typed in the original report is still not known; if the new check does not show the missing card, a new report with the exact text is needed.

46.4 Tests (expected, NOT run)
- Unit: 6 new in QuestionSearchRulesTest -> 95 of 95 (89 + 6).
- Emulator: QuestionEditDraftTest 7 + QuestionSearchTest 3 -> 78 (68 + 10).
- identityHash 786b471dfa14c9ae4739bb81479bab25 unchanged (no schema change); same debug key.
- The Kotlin code has NOT been compiled or run (no Gradle in the assistant environment). Like runs 37 and 39, the first CI run may show a compile error; send the report and the plan will record it.

46.5 Phone check list
10 steps in VERSION_NOTES_0.2.29.md (restore after kill, old text back = no draft, dialog and discard, save removes the draft, two cards separate, refused save keeps the draft, hashtag search).

46.6 Package order (unchanged from 45.4)
v0.2.29 (written, UNCONFIRMED) -> v0.2.30 editor fields (Minimum / Full modes, ratings with rule U-1, boards, years, reference, editable marks, undo/redo, duplicate warning) -> v0.2.31 delete + selection -> v0.2.32 JSON paste import -> the rest as in 43.4.

46.7 UI inventory status (39.6)
 UI-05 In-topic search: CONFIRMED (v0.2.27); hashtag search WRITTEN (v0.2.29, UNCONFIRMED); board / year / solution search OPEN.
 UI-11 Edit existing question: core CONFIRMED (v0.2.28); drafts WRITTEN (v0.2.29, UNCONFIRMED); delete part v0.2.31.

52.2 Part C-2, what was written (UI-08, screens)
- Picker: a dialog with the board list (built-in + custom, archived ones left out), a year window of the 12 most recent years (newest first, from the phone's current year; BoardRules.yearWindow) and "অন্য সাল" (typed with Bengali or Latin digits, BoardRules.parseYear; error text while it is not a valid year). The add button is on only for a valid, new board + year pair.
- Chips with remove in the editor (BoardTagsEditor): one editor per block (stimulus and ক..ঘ) on the new-question screen and the edit screen; tap a chip to remove it; at most 30 tags per block. Tag controls show only when the block has text (same rule as ratings, rule B-9).
- "নতুন বোর্ড" dialog (code + name) with a message for each refusal (AddBoardResult); the new board is selected at once.
- Card: the light chip row shows the stimulus tags (or the tags of a single question); tags of the sub-questions of a group with a stimulus show under their heading; "ট্যাগ নেই" only when nothing on the card is tagged (pure rules in BoardLabels.kt).
- Drafts: DraftText has stimulusBoards and boards; the draft JSON has "stimulusBoards" and "boards" ([{"b":code,"y":year}]); an older draft without them loads as "no tags"; bad entries (empty code, year out of range, repeated tag, more than 30) are skipped on load. Tags alone are not draft content (isBlank unchanged). The same change detection as ratings: a tags-only edit counts as a change (save button, discard question).
- ViewModels: QuestionEditorViewModel and QuestionEditViewModel carry the tags, pass them to saveGroup / updateGroup (the C-1 rule layer is unchanged) and expose the catalog (boardCatalog) and addBoard; QuestionListViewModel exposes the catalog for the card chips.
- No domain / DAO / schema change: identityHash must stay 786b471d... , database version 1. versionCode 33, versionName 0.2.30c2-boards-screens. No new dependency.

52.3 DECISIONS by the assistant (owner may overrule)

 B-6  Tapping a chip in the editor removes it at once, no question.
 B-7  Year window = 12 most recent years.
 B-8  Archived boards are not offered in the picker; the picker closes after each added tag.
 B-9  A block without text stores no tags, so its tag controls are hidden until it has text.

53. v0.2.30c-2 CONFIRMED; PART C-3 (SEARCH BY BOARD AND YEAR) WRITTEN - VERSION 1.44
============================================================

Planning update date: 18 November 2026

53.1 Basis
- Owner report for QuestionBank_v0_2_30c2.zip: CI clean, instrumented (emulator) test clean, check list clean. The report files and the step-by-step results of the check list were NOT sent this time (same as for v0.2.30b-fix1), so the counts (expected 132 unit / 96 emulator) and the 8 phone steps were not checked by the assistant. v0.2.30c-2 is CONFIRMED by owner report only.
- The owner asked for the next step: C-3 (50.2), the last part of v0.2.30c.

53.2 Decision at the start of C-3 (50.2 left it open: "the hashtag-style rule or a board chip in the search")
- A filter bar with two chips (board, year) under the search box. Reasons: the board and the year are catalog values, not words; typing them as words would clash with question text, with the FTS tokenizer and with Bengali / Latin digits (P-46); chips make the search explicit and let board + year be tested as ONE tag.
- Rules C3-1 to C3-6 are listed in VERSION_NOTES_0.2.30c3.md: filter chips, not text words (C3-1); board + year on the same tag (C3-2); a stimulus tag counts for all sub-questions of the card, a question tag for that question (C3-3); with a filter on, text AND tags must fit the same question and only those sub-questions are shown, without a filter the old behaviour is unchanged (C3-4); closing the search clears text and filter (C3-5); text without a usable word is still NoTerms, also with a filter (C3-6).

53.3 Part C-3, what was written
- Domain: BoardFilter(boardCode?, year?) with isEmpty and matches(tags); QuestionSearchRules.filterByBoards(groups, textMatchedQuestionIds?, textMatchedStimulusIds, filter) (pure).
- Repository: search(topicId, rawQuery, filter: BoardFilter? = null). Without a filter the code path and the result are the same as in v0.2.29; with a filter and no text the tags alone decide; text and filter are combined by filterByBoards. No new query: the tags were already read for the list (tagsOfTopic).
- UI: BoardFilterBar (two chips, board dialog, year dialog with the year window and "অন্য সাল", "ফিল্টার মুছুন"); QuestionListViewModel has `filter` (changeFilter) and a 3-flow combine, so a filter change searches at once; the list screen shows search results also while only the filter is on; the back arrow clears both.
- No schema, DAO or entity change: identityHash must stay 786b471d... , database version 1. versionCode 34, versionName 0.2.30c3-board-search. No new dependency.
- Lessons of P-54 / P-55 applied: no new sealed subtype; the only changed signature (search) has a default parameter, and every caller was grepped (list ViewModel and tests).

53.4 Tests (expected, NOT run)
- Unit: 10 new in BoardFilterSearchTest -> 142 of 142 (132 + 10).

54.2 Schema check for part D (rule 39.5), against the real entities and the v1.json schema
- type_cq_stimuli.reference_text TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '' : exists. No CHECK on it.
- type_cq_questions.marks REAL NOT NULL : exists; the CHECK emulation trigger requires marks > 0.
- type_cq_questions.question_type_id TEXT NOT NULL, foreign key to type_cq_question_types (ON DELETE RESTRICT) : exists. Seeded types: qt_knowledge, qt_comprehension, qt_application, qt_higher_order (CqLabel).
- RESULT: no migration, no new column, no new table. identityHash must stay 786b471d... , database version 1.
- Open question inside D (not a schema question): the type chooser. Today the type is fixed by the label (ক = knowledge, ... ঘ = higher order) and marks follow the label (1, 2, 3, 4). Plan 2686 says "type chooser and editable marks for each question", and the Minimum mode (2682) says "marks follow the type while at the default". Decision D-0 below.

54.3 Decision D-0 (assistant, owner may overrule) - what "type chooser" means
- A question keeps its label (ক..ঘ). The TYPE of a question becomes changeable among the four seeded types; the marks DEFAULT follows the type (knowledge 1, comprehension 2, application 3, higher order 4) until the owner types other marks for that question ("marks follow the type while at the default").
- Marks are decimal and greater than 0 (plan 423: keep raw marks input as text until validation, no integer truncation). Upper limit 100 (assistant choice; stops typing errors).
- Reference text belongs to the stimulus only (the column is on type_cq_stimuli). A single question without a stimulus has no reference text. Limit: same text limit as the stimulus text.

Decision proposed (owner may overrule): v0.2.30d-1..3 stay PENDING and are built after Phase 5 is verified; "delete + selection" in 43.4 moves to the next free number after the reference-data package.

Risk: the first CI run may show several compile errors at once. Action: push and run CI BEFORE any new feature code; fix diagnostics one by one.
 P-58  FIXED in WIP2. Renaming a category to an existing name made SQLite raise a UNIQUE violation (reproduced in SQLite) that no code caught; the exception left a coroutine in the screen and would crash the app. Now renameCategory / renameTypeCategory / renameChapterCategory and both reassign calls return false on a refused write (new safeWrite helper, cancellation is never swallowed).

 P-62  OPEN (design guard, no change). TypeCqReferenceDao.replaceQuestionBoards rejects archived boards. The current editor does not use it (TypeCqQuestionRepositoryImpl only checks that a board code exists), so questions that keep an old archived-board tag can still be edited. If an editor is ever switched to the generic path, keep existing tags of archived boards allowed, otherwise archiving a board would block editing old questions. Add a test then.

============================================================ 58. UNIFIED PHASE 6-8 EXECUTION TRACK AND STATUS LEDGER (v1.49)
============================================================

58.1 User-approved direction and governing rule
- Phase 6 (Editor State Engine), Phase 7 (Minimum Editor), and Phase 8 (Full Editor) are to be treated as ONE coordinated editor implementation track, using ONE shared EditorDraft/save architecture.
- The implementation must cover both Type_CQ and Chapter_CQ, while preserving their separate repository/data boundaries.
- “Together” means one coordinated design and integration effort, not three disconnected editor implementations. It does NOT mean that unfinished code or unverified tests may be called complete.
- To avoid delivering three incompatible partial editors, the preferred delivery is one integrated Phase 6-8 source package after the implementation milestones and their internal tests are complete. No new version number is assigned yet because P-56 is still open.
- The hard prerequisite remains the Phase 4/5 WIP2 gate in 57.6. No new editor-feature implementation is to be stacked on top of the currently unverified WIP2 package until the owner has pushed it, CI/build and instrumented workflows are green, the identityHash is confirmed, and the owner phone checklist passes. This preserves rule 31.4 and addresses P-57.

58.2 Status vocabulary (use these labels in every future update)
- DONE / VERIFIED: implementation exists AND the required automated checks and owner/device checks have passed; cite the report or checklist evidence.
- PARTIAL / VERIFIED: only the stated subset is confirmed; the remaining listed scope is still open.
- WRITTEN / UNVERIFIED: source or tests have been written, but the relevant Gradle/KSP/CI/emulator/phone verification has not passed or has not been supplied.
- STATIC-CHECKED ONLY: SQL/source-level inspection or replay only; this is NOT a build, Room, emulator, or phone pass.
- NOT STARTED: no implementation is present for the stated feature.
- BLOCKED: cannot safely proceed until a named dependency, decision, or failed gate is resolved.
- TEST PENDING: implementation may exist, but the specified test has not yet been run or evidenced.
Rule: implementation status and test status must be recorded in separate columns. “Code written” must never be converted to “tested”, and an expected test count must never be reported as an actual test result.

58.3 Baseline audit from the supplied v0.2.31-phase5-wip2 source ZIP
Audit source: QuestionBank_v0_2_31_PHASE5_WIP2.zip; its BUILD_STATUS.txt; app/build.gradle.kts; QuestionBankDatabase.kt; canonical SQL schema; EditorDraftRepository/EditorUseCases; QuestionEditorViewModel/QuestionEditViewModel; QuestionEditorScreen/QuestionEditScreen; existing unit and androidTest source files.

A. Build/test baseline
- CURRENT WORKING SOURCE: v0.2.31-phase5-wip2, versionCode 36.
- CURRENT PACKAGE STATUS: WRITTEN / UNVERIFIED. BUILD_STATUS.txt explicitly says “NOT COMPILED, NOT RUN”; Gradle 8.14.3 could not be obtained in the offline environment.
- LAST CONFIRMED BUILD BASELINE: v0.2.30c3, CI run #47, 142/142 unit tests and 99/99 emulator tests. These counts belong to that older baseline only; they are NOT test results for WIP1/WIP2.
- Phase 4 WIP1 + Phase 5 WIP1/WIP2 are stacked on that baseline and remain unverified. Expected 145 unit / 109 emulator counts in v1.48 are estimates only until CI reports actual totals.
- No claim is made here that WIP2 has passed Kotlin compilation, KSP/Room generation, unit tests, emulator instrumentation, or owner phone checks.

B. Relevant existing implementation found in the ZIP
- New-question draft: QuestionEditorViewModel + DraftText; draft writer uses an application-scope queue, and successful save removes the new-question draft in the repository transaction. Existing tests cover several draft save/load/reopen/failure scenarios.
- Edit draft: QuestionEditViewModel; edit drafts are separately saved/restored and have tests for draft recovery and successful-save/discard behavior.
- Generic draft groundwork: EditorDraft, ValidateEditorDraft, LoadEditorDraft, SaveEditorDraft, DiscardEditorDraft, and EditorDraftRepositoryImpl exist. The repository stores opaque JSON in app_metadata. This groundwork is NOT yet the unified multi-block editor state required by Phase 6; the current ViewModels still use their separate DraftText/edit-draft pathways. P-55.4 remains open until the boundary and backward-compatible reconciliation are designed and tested.
- Duplicate-check groundwork: CheckDuplicateQuestion calls repository isDuplicate methods. The plan's current status remains “repository check only”; the UI warning, policy, timing, and tests are not complete.
- Reference/marks/type groundwork: the canonical SQL and entities already contain stimulus reference_text, REAL/Double marks, and Type_CQ question_type_id. The current database version is 1. These fields do not by themselves prove the v0.2.30d save/edit/read-back/draft UI work is complete.
- Current Type_CQ editor displays seeded question-type labels and fixed CqLabel marks/type mapping. A user-editable per-question type chooser and editable marks are not present in the inspected editor screens/ViewModels. Chapter_CQ schema currently has marks and stimulus reference_text but does not have the Type_CQ question_type_id column. P-32/D-0 therefore remains a decision/semantics item; do not silently add Type_CQ-only fields to Chapter_CQ.
- Full-editor storage groundwork exists in schema/entities for image metadata, stimulus-image links, and ordered TEXT/IMAGE solution parts. In the inspected source, the full image import/processing/replacement and ordered solution editor UI are not implemented. Phase 10 media lifecycle work remains a dependency; schema tables alone are not a working media pipeline.
- app/build.gradle.kts currently contains AndroidX test core/runner/ext-junit only. It does NOT contain androidx.compose.ui:ui-test-junit4 or ui-test-manifest. P-63 remains open; Compose UI automation must not be claimed as present.
