58.4 Unified target architecture (must be agreed before implementation)
- One shared EditorDraft model for the editor session, with scope TYPE_CQ / CHAPTER_CQ and stable identifiers for the stimulus and every question block.
- The draft must be group-based, not a single-question-only object: stimulus text/reference/rating/boards; ordered question blocks; label and stable question ID; raw marks input; text and short answer; type ID only where the relevant domain/schema supports it; rating; board/year links; categories; ordered solution parts; ordered image references and placement; deleted IDs; editor mode; dirty/validation/saving state.
- Keep raw marks as String while typing. Parse and validate at the domain/save boundary; persist as the schema's REAL/Double. Do not truncate decimal marks. Current canonical schema rule is marks > 0; a maximum must not be invented without an approved requirement.
- Reference text belongs to the stimulus in the current canonical schema (type_cq_stimuli.reference_text and chapter_cq_stimuli.reference_text), not independently to each sub-question.
- Keep editor mode as UI/session state, not as a new persisted question property. Minimum is the default whenever a new editor session opens. Switching modes must retain all hidden Full fields, images, solution parts, relationships, and unsaved text.
- Keep the Type_CQ and Chapter_CQ repository boundaries separate. Use adapters/use cases over their separate repositories; UI must never call DAOs directly.
- Resolve P-55.4 before wiring the generic draft store: choose one canonical editor draft schema and provide a backward-compatible conversion/restore path for existing DraftText and edit-draft JSON. Do not discard unknown/hidden fields while decoding or re-saving older drafts.
- Use one save command/use-case boundary with transaction-safe per-scope repository operations. Successful content save and removal of its associated draft must remain atomic. For images, coordinate file operations and DB metadata through the existing operation-journal design; no orphan file or dangling DB reference is acceptable.
- Mode changes must not create duplicate question IDs, reset question order, or rewrite stable IDs. Save must be idempotent against repeated taps.

58.5 Integrated implementation milestones (one Phase 6-8 track)
M0 — Prerequisite gate (BLOCKED until complete)
1. Owner pushes v0.2.31-phase5-wip2 and runs both build and instrumented workflows.
2. Fix compile/KSP failures one by one; rerun until green; confirm Room identityHash and database version match the intended v1.48 baseline.
3. Owner performs the exact v1.48 Reference Data phone checklist in 57.6. Confirm reference labels/boards/categories and safe reassignment behavior.
4. Resolve Phase 4 generic-draft/domain boundary issue (P-55.4) sufficiently to ensure the Editor feature will not depend on an unverified conflicting repository design.
Exit: Phase 4/5 may be marked DONE only after all gates pass. Until then, Phase 6-8 feature implementation is BLOCKED.

M1 — Editor data/rules integration (includes the intent of v0.2.30d-1..3; no screen behavior is declared complete until tested)
- Persist/read back reference text, editable decimal marks, and the selected question type where supported; include them in new and edit drafts.
- Existing schema already contains stimulus reference_text and marks; Type_CQ has question_type_id. First compare Room entities, canonical SQL, and the exported schema. Do not add a migration or bump the DB version if the existing schema already supports the exact fields. If a field/relationship is genuinely missing, add a reviewed Room migration and v1-to-new-version migration tests before using it.
- Preserve old draft JSON and old call sites with safe defaults/compatibility adapters; no new sealed result subtype unless all exhaustive when callers are updated.
- Type/marks semantics: use P-32's D-0 proposal as provisional only: ক/খ/গ/ঘ labels stay fixed; Type_CQ type may be selected from the four seeded semantic types; marks follow the type only while untouched/default, and an explicit user-entered mark remains unchanged when type changes. Confirm the Chapter_CQ rule before adding any type field there.
- Test save, edit-only change, clearing/blank behavior, read-back, draft round-trip, old draft restore, malformed/raw mark rejection, and decimal preservation.

M2 — Phase 6 shared Editor State Engine
- Implement immutable or safely controlled EditorDraft updates through explicit EditorEvent actions and a single observable EditorState.
- Include load, reset, new/edit initialization, field-level validation, dirty tracking, error map, scroll-to-first-error request, saving state, save de-duplication, Back/unsaved-change handling, restore/recovery, and successful-save/discard cleanup.
- Implement Undo/Redo for all draft-visible content. Group rapid typing into sensible edit units; after undo then a new edit, discard the redo branch. Keep stable IDs/order. History cap remains an owner/design decision; choose a bounded policy only after testing memory/performance, and log the chosen cap.
- Connect duplicate checking to a non-blocking warning surface; define exact normalization/scope and whether it checks current scope/topic, stimulus or only question text. Default policy should be advisory only unless the owner approves blocking. Do not present repository-level isDuplicate as a completed UI feature.
- Test every state transition in JVM tests, including rapid input, grouped typing, undo/redo boundaries, redo-branch truncation, mode switch preservation, repeated Save, failed Save draft retention, process death/restart recovery, and two independent drafts.

M3 — Phase 7 Minimum Editor for both scopes
- Deliver working New Question and Edit Question flows with the core fields: scope/path, stimulus, reference text, question blocks, labels, editable marks, type selection where permitted, short answer, ratings, board/year and category selection, save, validation, dirty indicator, close/back and unsaved-change confirmation, loading/error/empty states.
- Minimum is always the default on opening a fresh editor session. Full-only content already present in a draft must stay intact when hidden in Minimum.
- Provide essential image controls only when the media add/read/remove path is transactional and has passed the media checks; otherwise do not expose a control that can lose or orphan files. Full top/bottom placement and advanced image order belong to M4.
- Implement keyboard/focus behavior and scroll-to-first-invalid-field.
- Test create/edit/save/close/reopen for both Type_CQ and Chapter_CQ, with old data and old drafts. Confirm repository/read-back and FTS consistency.

M4 — Phase 8 Full Editor for both scopes
- Add all Minimum controls plus complete stimulus metadata, top/bottom image placement, image ordering/replacement, multiple board/year tags, multiple categories, ordered question-block add/remove/reorder, ordered text/image solution parts, mathematical content toolbar, legacy solution-marker import, live preview, and full validation.
- Keep all question blocks and solution/image order stable through mode switches, undo/redo, failed writes, save/reload, process restart, backup/restore, and media recovery.
- Build image picker/import, processing, verification/hash, replacement, missing/corrupt-state display, and lifecycle coordination to the required depth. This work is a scoped dependency on Phase 10 and must not cause Phase 10 to be marked globally DONE unless its full scope also passes.
- Integrate with the existing operation journal and atomic save boundary; test crash/interruption points so DB metadata and files cannot diverge.
- Test full editor flows for Type_CQ and Chapter_CQ independently, including long content, multiple blocks, images, solution ordering, math preview failure handling, and restore after process death.

Integrated delivery rule
- Keep M0-M4 as internal milestones on the same coordinated Phase 6-8 implementation track. Do not issue separate feature ZIPs that strand the user with an incomplete Minimum/Full editor. Run unit/contract tests continuously at each milestone. Once M0 is green and M1-M4 are integrated, create ONE combined Phase 6-8 candidate package, run the complete CI/build and instrumented suite, and give the owner one phone-checklist candidate. If any gate fails, fix and rerun that same candidate before calling any of Phases 6-8 DONE.
- This unified delivery decision does not waive the prior M0 Phase 4/5 gate. It also does not waive CI/emulator/phone acceptance for the combined candidate.
- Package version is TBD. Do not reuse v0.2.31 (already used by Phase 5 WIP2), and do not silently collide with the historic delete+selection / JSON import sequence. Update P-56 with an explicit version map before producing the candidate ZIP.

60.3 Implementation ledger — Phase 6: Editor State Engine
Status: PARTIAL / SOURCE WRITTEN / NOT COMPILED OR VERIFIED.
- Added `EditorHistory<T>`: bounded history (80 entries), Undo/Redo, redo-branch truncation after a new change, and same-field coalescing within 700 ms.
- Type_CQ New/Edit ViewModels and UI now expose Undo/Redo and Minimum/Full mode state. Chapter_CQ editor also uses the history helper and mode selection. A Type_CQ history snapshot covers text, answers, ratings, board/year tags, reference, question types, marks and text-solution parts; Chapter_CQ snapshot covers its form fields, categories, solution text and mode.
- Added duplicate warning flow for Type_CQ New using the exact-text topic lookup (save is disabled while the asynchronous check is running; user may confirm save anyway). Chapter_CQ New/Edit performs an exact-text chapter lookup excluding the current question and offers a “save anyway” confirmation. Type_CQ Edit duplicate warning is still pending.
- Extended Type_CQ legacy DraftText version-2 JSON with reference text, question types, marks and solution text, with defaulted decode behavior for old drafts. Chapter_CQ draft state is persisted through the generic `EditorDraftRepository` as opaque JSON.
- P-55.4 is NOT resolved: Type_CQ still uses its established DraftText/edit-draft store, while Chapter_CQ uses the generic EditorDraftRepository. These are not yet one canonical cross-scope draft schema/state engine. A migration or reconciliation is still required before marking Phase 6 DONE.
- Editor mode is a UI mode, not a persisted question property. Type_CQ mode switching is not included in the DraftText history payload; content Undo/Redo works on the DraftText snapshot. No claim is made that all mode changes are undoable.

60.4 Implementation ledger — Phase 7: Minimum Editor
Status: PARTIAL / SOURCE WRITTEN / NOT COMPILED OR VERIFIED.
- Type_CQ New/Edit now include stimulus reference text, existing ratings, board/year, editable positive decimal marks and selectable active question types in Full mode. The default type and marks remain the CqLabel defaults; the label itself remains ক/খ/গ/ঘ. Reference text is stored with the stimulus. Existing Type_CQ text-only and older rating/board slices remain as previously recorded; this new combined candidate has not been tested.
- Chapter_CQ placeholder has been replaced with a dedicated workspace. It provides subject/chapter browse, add/rename, question create/edit, stimulus/reference, question label, positive decimal marks, short answer, ratings, one primary board/year control and category assignment. Chapter_CQ intentionally has no question_type_id column or Type_CQ question-type picker; no Type_CQ field has been added to Chapter_CQ.
- Chapter_CQ save now writes the optional stimulus plus question, solution, board and category rows inside a Room transaction and compares stored rows on read-back. Existing archived board/category links can be retained on edit; new assignments still require active references.
- Existing additional Chapter_CQ board/year links are preserved during an edit, but the UI only exposes the first/primary board/year value for editing. Chapter_CQ category management create/rename/archive is not implemented in this workspace.
- No device evidence exists for this new UI; all added screens and saves remain unverified.

60.5 Implementation ledger — Phase 8: Full Editor
Status: PARTIAL / SOURCE WRITTEN / NOT COMPILED OR VERIFIED.
- Added ordered text solution parts in Type_CQ New/Edit and Chapter_CQ Full mode, with add/remove/reorder controls and draft preservation. Type_CQ DAO text-solution replacement retains existing non-TEXT/image rows and reorders them after the text rows; Chapter_CQ edit carries forward existing IMAGE solution rows while replacing text rows.
- Added a lightweight math-symbol bar (√, π, ×, ÷, ±, ≤, ≥, ∞, ², ³, parentheses/braces, ∑, ∫) and plain-text preview for the Full mode editor.
- The symbol bar appends at the end of the field value; it is not selection/cursor-aware. Preview displays raw text; there is no LaTeX/MathJax/equation renderer.
- Image/media selection, import/upload, crop, replace, position/reorder, stimulus-image management and image+text mixed solution editing are NOT implemented. Existing IMAGE solution rows are only preserved where the text editor touches those records. Phase 10 media pipeline remains a prerequisite for those capabilities.

============================================================ 62. STATIC SOURCE RECHECK AND CORRECTIONS (v1.53)

62.1 Review scope and rules
- Rechecked the Phase 6–8 candidate against the original `QuestionBank_v0_2_31_PHASE5_WIP2.zip` source tree.
- Compared source-tree contents and inspected changed Type_CQ / Chapter_CQ DAO, repository, navigation, ViewModel and screen files, together with BUILD_STATUS and the v1.52 phase ledger.
- No Android build, Kotlin compilation, KSP generation, unit test, instrumented test, emulator test or phone test was run. The owner explicitly instructed that tests are not required for this iteration.
- ZIP container integrity checking is not an app test and does not prove that Android source compiles or runs.

62.2 Defects found and corrected during review
1. Type_CQ duplicate checker repository mismatch
   - The Type_CQ New route referred to `container.typeCqQuestions.isDuplicate(...)`, but `typeCqQuestions` is a `TypeCqQuestionRepository` without that member.
   - `isDuplicate(topicId, questionText)` is declared on `TypeCqRepository`, exposed by `container.typeCqData`.
   - Corrected the route to call `container.typeCqData.isDuplicate(topicId, text)`. Static interface review indicates the earlier call would fail Kotlin compilation. The correction is NOT compiled.
2. Archived Type_CQ question-type retention
   - `TypeCqQuestionDao.updateGroup` previously rejected a metadata update whenever a question type was archived, even when the update retained the same already-stored type and changed only marks.
   - Added `questionTypeOf(questionId)` and allow an archived type only when it is already the stored type for that same question. New/changed assignments to archived types remain refused.
   - This correction is NOT Room/KSP-compiled or runtime-tested.
3. Minimum / Full mode visibility
   - The ordered text-solution editing controls (solution fields and add/remove/reorder actions) are now gated behind `EditorMode.FULL` on both Type_CQ New and Edit screens.
   - The visibility gate does not clear the ViewModel's existing solution draft values; retention through mode switches is still UNVERIFIED.
4. Duplicate warning semantics
   - Type_CQ duplicate lookup uses exact trimmed question-text equality, not fuzzy similarity. The New editor warning copy now says that exactly identical wording was found; no fuzzy duplicate detector is claimed.
5. Chapter_CQ save transaction boundary
   - Chapter_CQ DAO save validates that an edited question still belongs to the selected chapter and performs question/stimulus/solution/board/category read-back checks before its transactional method returns. A mismatch throws inside the transaction so the outer transaction can roll back.
   - Removed a redundant repository-level read-back performed after the DAO transaction had committed. That post-commit check could have returned `false` after data had already been written. The repository now returns the DAO transaction result and maps exceptions to failure.
   - Room/KSP compile and runtime rollback behaviour remain UNVERIFIED because the user asked not to run tests.

62.3 Corrected package and plan status
| Area | Implementation status | Verification status |
|---|---|---|
| Static source recheck | COMPLETED for the inspected scope | Static inspection only |
| Defects listed in 62.2 | SOURCE CORRECTED | NOT COMPILED / NOT TESTED |
| Phase 6 | PARTIAL | NOT COMPILED / NOT VERIFIED |
| Phase 7 | PARTIAL | NOT COMPILED / NOT VERIFIED |
| Phase 8 | PARTIAL | NOT COMPILED / NOT VERIFIED |
| Phase 4/5 M0 | NOT PASSED | WIP2 baseline still unverified |
| Room schema / identityHash | No schema change declared | NOT checked with Room/KSP |

============================================================ 63. DEEP STATIC RECHECK AND DATA-SAFETY CORRECTIONS (v1.54)

63.1 Review scope and constraints
- Reopened and directly inspected the candidate ZIP, `PROJECT_PLAN_v1_53_UPDATED.txt`, `STATIC_RECHECK_REPORT_v1_53.txt`, `BUILD_STATUS.txt`, and the Version Notes. The v1.53 report is superseded by this section where findings differ.
- Compared the source tree against the original `QuestionBank_v0_2_31_PHASE5_WIP2.zip` baseline. All 235 baseline file paths are preserved; no original path was removed. Before adding this v1.54 plan/report to the package, the candidate contained 243 files. Fifteen pre-existing source files were changed and three new Kotlin UI/helper source files were present.
- Manually reviewed the changed Type_CQ and Chapter_CQ DAO/repository/ViewModel/screen/navigation paths, including save/draft ordering, cancellation, read-back placement, archived-reference behavior, board/year link updates, and preservation of non-text solution rows.
- Per the owner's explicit instruction, NO Gradle build, Kotlin compile, KSP generation, unit test, instrumented test, emulator test or phone test was run. ZIP structure checks performed after packaging are archive-integrity checks only, not application tests.

63.2 Additional defects found after v1.53 and source corrections
1. Chapter_CQ rapid duplicate-save possibility
   - The save action could previously be triggered more than once before Compose had rendered the loading state. A synchronous `savingQuestion`/`editorLoading` guard now prevents launching a second save operation and the editor's save/dismiss actions are disabled while saving.
2. Chapter_CQ draft write/save race and non-atomic draft cleanup
   - Delayed application-scope draft jobs could race with Save. In addition, deleting the draft after a successful question save in a separate operation could leave a stale draft if cleanup failed.
   - Added a process-wide owner-keyed draft write coordinator with a shared `Mutex`, revision checks, cancellation/join of the pending writer, and an explicit final-snapshot write before save. The Chapter_CQ question, optional stimulus, solution/board/category rows, read-back checks and draft-key deletion now run in one Room transaction. A rollback leaves the draft key intact.
3. Type_CQ duplicate-check cancellation and stale-result race
   - Cancellation is now rethrown rather than treated as an ordinary lookup error. A job-identity check and active-context check prevent an older cancelled duplicate query from overwriting the warning or checking indicator for a newer query.
4. Type_CQ save read-back after commit
   - New and Edit repository paths no longer perform a second validation/read-back after the DAO transaction. Insert/update read-back checks now run before draft deletion inside the Type_CQ DAO transaction, so a mismatch throws before commit and the draft is not removed.
5. Mixed text/image solution position preservation
   - Type_CQ text-only solution replacement and Chapter_CQ text-solution editing now retain existing non-TEXT rows (including image rows) at their prior positions. New text parts append after the prior maximum position. The current editor still does not expose image editing; preservation is not an image-editor implementation.
6. Chapter_CQ nested transaction dependency
   - The outer stimulus + question save previously called another public `@Transaction` DAO method. The shared write body is now a private non-transactional DAO helper called by the public transactional wrappers, keeping the whole stimulus/question/draft operation in one transaction boundary rather than relying on nested transaction behavior.
7. Chapter_CQ edit target disappearing during an open editor
   - The repository API now distinguishes “must update existing” from “insert new”; DAO validation refuses an update if the target row no longer exists and refuses a new insert if the ID already exists. The editor load path clears stale form fields and blocks saving when the requested question has disappeared.
8. Chapter_CQ shared stimulus cross-question contamination
   - A question-level edit could previously change a stimulus row shared by other questions. If stimulus content changes while that stimulus is shared, the DAO now copies the stimulus and its existing stimulus-image links, then attaches only the edited question to the clone. Unchanged shared stimuli remain shared.
9. Chapter_CQ primary board/year replacement
   - Changing the primary board/year previously retained the old primary entry as a secondary entry. The screen now replaces the former primary and keeps the other secondary entries, removing a secondary duplicate if it matches the new primary.
10. Cancellation propagation and navigation polish
   - Chapter_CQ repository subject/chapter add/rename methods now rethrow coroutine cancellation. The Chapter_CQ root header no longer displays a dead back arrow with an empty callback.

63.3 Additional static cross-checks
- Confirmed repository/interface call sites for the new `mustUpdateExisting` argument and Chapter_CQ workspace wiring by source inspection.
- Confirmed the Type_CQ duplicate-check call is through `container.typeCqData` (the repository interface that declares `isDuplicate`), not the question repository.
- Confirmed the Type_CQ archived question-type guard permits retaining the same archived type on unrelated edits but blocks a new/changed inactive type assignment.
- Confirmed `EditorMode.FULL` gates the solution editing controls; the controls being hidden does not claim that image/media features exist.

============================================================ 65. CI RUN 52 FAILURE AND FIX (v1.56)
============================================================

65.1 Evidence
- QuestionBank-ci-report, workflow "Android Test and APK Build", run 52, run ID 44474500375, commit 4442badfec85b3ff3e2b70fa357ef1460e9edc19, branch main, Java 17, Gradle exit code 1.

65.2 Compiler errors (4 lines, 1 defect, same message)
    QuestionEditViewModel.kt:94:9   Platform declaration clash (setEditorMode(EditorMode))
    QuestionEditViewModel.kt:295:5  Platform declaration clash (setEditorMode(EditorMode))
    QuestionEditorViewModel.kt:74:9 Platform declaration clash (setEditorMode(EditorMode))
    QuestionEditorViewModel.kt:164:5 Platform declaration clash (setEditorMode(EditorMode))

65.3 Cause (read from source)
- Both ViewModels declare "var editorMode by mutableStateOf(EditorMode.MINIMUM)" (public setter, so Kotlin generates setEditorMode(EditorMode)) and also a hand-written "fun setEditorMode(mode: EditorMode) { editorMode = mode }". Both compile to the same JVM method signature, which Kotlin rejects.

65.4 Fix (applied in the v1.56 ZIP)
- In QuestionEditViewModel.kt (line 93-94) and QuestionEditorViewModel.kt (line 73-74): add "private set" under the editorMode property. The explicit setEditorMode() function stays and is the only way to change the mode from outside.
- Callers checked: QuestionEditorScreen.kt (lines 186-188) and QuestionEditScreen.kt (lines 144-145) only read viewModel.editorMode and call viewModel.setEditorMode(mode); they are unaffected. The "editorMode = ..." lines in ChapterCqWorkspaceScreen.kt are local Compose state, not a ViewModel property, and are unaffected.
- Status: FIX APPLIED, UNVERIFIED until a CI run compiles it. A search of the source found no other property/function pair with this get/set pattern, but the compiler decides.

65.5 Not reached yet
- Kotlin stopped at this stage again: unit tests, androidTest compile, release build and emulator tests have still not run on the Phase 6-8 candidate. More errors may appear (P-57). Phase 4-8 stay WRITTEN / UNVERIFIED.

65.6 Problem log (new)
 P-66  CI run 52: Platform declaration clash on editorMode in both edit ViewModels. Status: FIX APPLIED, UNVERIFIED (65.4).
 Still open from before: P-18, P-32, P-55.4, P-56, P-57, P-62, P-63, P-65, release signing, Phase 3 migration work.

============================================================ 66. CI RUN 53 FAILURE AND CORRECTED FIX (v1.57)
============================================================

66.1 Evidence
- QuestionBank-ci-report, run 53, run ID 44475831018, commit ee511f9ca22d118651569eff84f755a2ec8297e2, Java 17, Gradle exit code 1. Failed task :app:compileDebugKotlin (same stop in androidTest compile and release steps).
- Exactly 2 compiler errors: QuestionEditViewModel.kt:95:17 and QuestionEditorViewModel.kt:75:17, "Syntax error: Expecting member declaration".

66.2 Cause (read from source) - the v1.56 fix was wrong
- The run 52 source already had "private set" under "var editorMode". The v1.56 fix added a second "private set" line, giving two consecutive "private set" lines, which is a syntax error. The v1.56 fix was written without checking the existing line. Lesson: read the exact lines before editing.
- The run 52 clash was real even with the existing "private set": Kotlin still reports a clash between the property setter <set-editorMode> and the explicit fun setEditorMode(mode), because both map to setEditorMode(EditorMode)V. So "private set" alone cannot be the fix.

66.3 Corrected fix (applied in the v1.57 ZIP)
- QuestionEditViewModel.kt and QuestionEditorViewModel.kt: the duplicate "private set" is removed (one "private set" remains under editorMode), and the explicit function is annotated: @JvmName("applyEditorMode") fun setEditorMode(mode: EditorMode) { editorMode = mode }. The JVM name of the function changes; the Kotlin name does not, so QuestionEditorScreen.kt and QuestionEditScreen.kt need no change. Nothing in the project calls setEditorMode from Java or reflection (checked by search).
- Status: FIX APPLIED, UNVERIFIED until a CI run compiles it. P-66 stays open; new P-67: v1.56 fix introduced a syntax error (fixed here).

66.4 Not reached yet
- Unit tests, androidTest compile, release build and emulator tests still have not run on the Phase 6-8 candidate. More errors may follow (P-57).

66.5 Next steps
1. Owner: replace the project with the v1.57 ZIP (or copy the two ViewModel files), commit, push, run the workflow, send the new CI report.
2. Then as in 65.7 step 2-3 and 64.9 steps 2-5.

============================================================ 67. PHASE 6–8 CANONICAL DRAFT INTEGRATION (v1.58)
============================================================

67.1 Owner direction and verification boundary
- Owner instructed that Phase 6, Phase 7 and Phase 8 should be implemented together and that no tests need to be run.
- Work in this update is source editing and static inspection only. No Gradle build, Kotlin/KSP compile, unit test, instrumented test, emulator test or phone test was run, following that instruction.
- The resulting source is therefore WRITTEN / UNVERIFIED. Do not infer that it compiles or works at runtime.
- The Phase 4/5 M0 prerequisite remains unpassed in the evidence available for this update. This editor work does not mark Phase 4/5 complete.

67.2 Files added
- `domain/editor/EditorSessionDraft.kt`: canonical schema-v2 model for shared Type_CQ / Chapter_CQ editing. It represents scope and owner, hierarchy IDs, stimulus/reference/rating/images/board-years, stable question-block IDs, raw marks text, question type, category IDs, and ordered TEXT/IMAGE solution parts. The JSON codec merges known values over the previous JSON and carries unknown object keys forward, including keyed nested arrays.
- `domain/editor/EditorStateMachine.kt`: immutable event/reducer model with loading/saving/dirty/validation/duplicate/save state, stimulus and block events, and bounded undo/redo history.
- `domain/editor/TypeCqDraftAdapter.kt`: compatibility conversion between the existing Type_CQ `DraftText` representation and the canonical schema, retaining prior hidden categories and image parts where the legacy model has no matching field.

67.3 Source changes made
- Type_CQ New and Edit ViewModels now load canonical schema-v2 drafts first, fall back to their prior legacy draft data, and write both the legacy recovery shape and canonical schema through their existing application-scope draft writer.
- The Type_CQ New and Edit save inputs now carry the canonical draft metadata key. The Room DAO clears that key inside the same transaction as the content save, after existing read-back checks have succeeded. The Edit VM also retains the legacy edit-draft recovery path on save failure.
- `AppNavHost` injects the shared `EditorDraftRepository` into the Type_CQ New and Edit ViewModels.
- Chapter_CQ converts its existing screen snapshot to the canonical schema-v2 representation when writing drafts and can restore canonical drafts while retaining compatibility with its earlier snapshot JSON.
- Existing application version metadata and Room schema version were not changed.

67.4 Updated status ledger
- Phase 6 — PARTIAL / WRITTEN / UNVERIFIED. A canonical shared schema, codec, reducer/event model and history helper now exist; Type_CQ New/Edit and Chapter_CQ draft paths now interoperate through the canonical JSON contract. However, the reducer is not yet wired as the single observable state source for both screen/ViewModel implementations, and existing per-screen state/history code remains. Back-navigation/unsaved-change handling is not complete across both scopes.
- Phase 7 — PARTIAL / WRITTEN / UNVERIFIED. Existing Minimum Editor functionality remains and canonical draft restore/save is added. Type_CQ category-assignment UI and a fully unified set of required validation/field semantics are still incomplete. Chapter_CQ still exposes its existing single-question-block editing surface.

The canonical model now has image references, placement/order, and mixed ordered text/image solution-part structures, and the adapters preserve existing image parts. This update does not add the complete image picker/import/replacement/placement UI or the required media-operation transaction/journal. Add/remove/reorder question-block UX, complete Chapter_CQ multi-tag editing, formula rendering and full rich preview/legacy solution-marker import remain incomplete.

============================================================ 68. REVIEW OF v1.58 AND TWO BUG FIXES (v1.59)
============================================================

68.1 Method and boundary
- Static source reading of the v1.58 ZIP only. No Kotlin compiler was available and no Gradle build, test or emulator run was made. All changes below are WRITTEN / UNVERIFIED.

68.2 Defects found and fixed
- P-68 (codec, serious): EditorSessionDraftCodec.encode used JSONObject.put(key, null). org.json removes the key on a null value, and mergeObject only iterates keys of the NEW object, so the OLD value from preservedJson survived. Clearing rating, questionTypeId, questionId, existingStimulusId, stimulusId, or a solution part text/imageId did not persist across restore. Fix: new private helper jsonOrNull(value) returns JSONObject.NULL for null; used at all eight nullable puts. decode already treats NULL as absent (isNull checks and the "null" string guard).
- P-69 (reducer, serious): EditorSessionReducer.reduce ignored every event except SaveSucceeded and ResetDirty while isSaving, which also dropped SaveFailed, so a failed save left isSaving=true forever. Fix: SaveFailed is allowed while saving (the comparison with setOf(...) is replaced by explicit type checks). The reducer is not yet wired into the UI, so no runtime symptom existed yet.

68.3 Tests added (written, not run)
- app/src/test/.../EditorSessionReducerTest.kt (pure JVM): failed save releases isSaving; edits are ignored while saving.
- app/src/androidTest/.../EditorSessionDraftCodecTest.kt (needs real org.json, so instrumented): clearing a rating persists; unknown keys survive a round trip.
- JVM unit tests must not use org.json unless testImplementation("org.json:json:<version>") is added; that was intentionally not changed.

68.3a Remaining low risks (not changed)
- Two EditorMode enums (ui.question and domain.editor; aliased imports avoid a clash) and two history classes (EditorHistory, EditorSessionHistory) can drift apart until P-55.4 unifies state ownership.
- decode returns null if any block is malformed, so the whole canonical draft is dropped and the legacy draft is used.

68.4 Plan assessment
- The plan is consistent and honest about PARTIAL / UNVERIFIED status. Open concerns: (1) CI runs 52 and 53 failed and the v1.57 fix has no CI result yet; run a CI compile on this ZIP before adding more code (compile is not a test run). (2) P-56 version map is still unresolved. (3) The plan file is very large and the ZIP carries copies of v1_48..v1_57; consider archiving them.

68.5 Next steps
1. Owner: replace the project with the v1.59 ZIP, commit, push, run CI, send the report.
2. Fix whatever CI reports, then continue the P-55.4 wiring.

68.6 Change log
- v1.59: P-68 and P-69 fixed; two test files written; app version metadata unchanged (versionCode=36, versionName=0.2.31-phase5-wip2).

END OF VERSION 1.59 UPDATE


============================================================ 69. SECOND REVIEW PASS (v1.60)
============================================================

69.1 Method and boundary
- Static reading of the Type_CQ New/Edit ViewModels, the Chapter_CQ screen draft code, the Type_CQ DAO/repository draft cleanup and the topic delete path. No compiler, build or test was run: all changes are WRITTEN / UNVERIFIED.

69.2 Defects found and fixed
- P-70 (Chapter_CQ, serious): toCanonicalDraft kept every previous board tag and only replaced the one with the same board code. After changing board A to B the draft held [A, B], and restore read the FIRST tag, so the form showed the old board A; a cleared board came back too. Fix: the form edits only the first tag (the same one it loads from the database); the new value is written first, the other previous tags are kept after it.
- P-71 (Type_CQ Edit, medium): TypeCqDraftAdapter matched existingQuestionIds by list position against the four labels. A card with only some of ক খ গ ঘ got the wrong question ID on a block. Fix: new optional parameter questionIdsByLabel (label letter to ID), passed by QuestionEditViewModel; the old positional behavior stays for callers that do not pass it.
- P-72 (Type_CQ New, medium): after a failed save the cancelled draft writer could leave an OLDER canonical draft, and restore prefers the canonical draft over the legacy one, so the newest typing could be lost after the process died. Fix: the failure branch also queues the snapshot to the new writer, which rewrites legacy and canonical.
- P-73 (Type_CQ New, low): after the person cleared all text, a blank canonical draft was still stored (because previous != null), and reopening showed a "draft restored" state for nothing. Fix: a blank draft without hidden categories, solution parts or images is discarded regardless of previous.
- P-74 (topic delete, low): deleting an empty topic removed only the legacy draft key and left draft:type_cq:editor:<topicId> orphaned in app_metadata. Fix: TypeCqTopicDao.deleteIfEmpty takes an optional editorDraftKey, deleted in the same transaction; TypeCqHierarchyRepositoryImpl passes it.

69.3 Test added (written, not run)
- app/src/test/.../TypeCqDraftAdapterTest.kt: question IDs follow labels for a card with only ক and গ.

69.4 Noted, not changed
- The key string "draft:type_cq:editor:<id>" is written by hand in three places (two ViewModels and the topic delete) next to EditorDraftRepositoryImpl.key(); a future rename must change all of them. A shared constant would be better.
- Chapter_CQ rating fields use toIntOrNull(); an invalid typed rating is stored as null and not restored as typed text.
- Changing the editor mode in Type_CQ New alone does not queue a draft write until the next edit.
- The Type_CQ Edit failure path was not reviewed for the same stale-canonical case as P-72.
- Still unchecked: the Compose screens (QuestionEditorScreen, QuestionEditScreen), AppNavHost wiring and the androidTest sources beyond the new test.

69.5 Next steps
1. Owner: replace the project with the v1.60 ZIP, commit, push, run CI, send the report.
2. Then review the Type_CQ Edit failure path and the P-55.4 wiring.

============================================================ 70. CI RUN 55 AND THE MISSING DEBUG KEYSTORE (v1.61)
============================================================

70.1 CI run 55 result
- Run 55 failed in the main Gradle step at :app:validateSigningDebug: "Keystore file .../app/debug.keystore not found for signing config 'debug'".
- Compiles succeeded in the same report: androidtest-compile.log (compileDebugKotlin and compileDebugAndroidTestKotlin, BUILD SUCCESSFUL) and release-assemble.log (compileReleaseKotlin, BUILD SUCCESSFUL). KSP/Room processing also passed.
- The debug unit tests (testDebugUnitTest) and the debug APK did not run, so their results are still unknown.
- Which source ZIP run 55 used is not stated in the report.

70.2 Cause
- app/debug.keystore (fixed debug signing key, committed on purpose since v0.2.23, see build.gradle.kts signingConfigs) is not in the ZIPs v1.58, v1.59, v1.60. Replacing the repository contents with a ZIP removed it from the repository.
- P-75: a project ZIP must never be used to replace the repository folder when binary files such as app/debug.keystore exist only in the repository. Copy the ZIP contents over the existing folder instead of deleting it first.

70.3 Fix in this package
- The original keystore could not be recovered here. A NEW fixed debug keystore was generated and placed at app/debug.keystore with the values build.gradle.kts expects: alias androiddebugkey, store and key password android, RSA 2048, 10000 days, CN=Android Debug.
- Consequence: its certificate differs from the earlier debug key. A phone that has an APK signed with the OLD key will refuse to install over it. Steps: export a backup in the app first (v0.2.24 backup export), uninstall, install the new APK, restore from the backup. After this one change the new key stays fixed for every later CI run.
- If the old keystore is found later (an old ZIP or an old commit), put it back at app/debug.keystore; installing over the old app then works again without uninstalling.
- The status of this key change is WRITTEN / UNVERIFIED until CI signs an APK with it.

70.4 Next steps
1. Owner: copy this ZIP's contents over the repository folder (do not delete the folder first), commit including app/debug.keystore, push, run CI, send the report.
2. Check that the unit tests (including EditorSessionReducerTest and TypeCqDraftAdapterTest) and the APK signing step pass.
3. Before installing the new APK over an app signed with the old key, make a backup export.

70.5 Change log
- v1.61: CI run 55 analysed; P-75 recorded; new app/debug.keystore added; version metadata unchanged (versionCode=36, versionName=0.2.31-phase5-wip2).

END OF VERSION 1.61 UPDATE



============================================================ 71. PHASE 9 ATOMIC EDITOR PERSISTENCE — IMPLEMENTATION SLICE (v1.62)
============================================================

71.1 Owner direction and verification boundary
- Continue Phase 9 using the v1.61 Phase 6–8 ZIP and v1.61 Project Plan as the baseline.
- The prior owner direction not to run tests was carried forward. No Gradle build, Kotlin/KSP/Room compile, unit test, instrumented test, APK build/signing, emulator run or device run was performed. This section records source changes and static source inspection only.
- Phase 9 remains PARTIAL / WRITTEN / UNVERIFIED. Do not infer compilation or runtime success.
- No Room schema, migration, database version, versionCode or versionName change was made.

71.2 Source changes written
- Added `domain/editor/EditorSaveCommand.kt` with factory-created immutable snapshots for Type_CQ create/edit and Chapter_CQ bundle saves. Nested collections are defensively copied and exposed as unmodifiable collections. The Room DAO remains the transaction boundary.
- Type_CQ save input now supports per-label category IDs for new questions, full category replacement for edited questions, and an explicit deleted-question ID set.
- `QuestionEditRules` validates that deletion IDs belong to the loaded target group, rejects deletes in a single-question scope, prevents a stimulus group from becoming empty, and produces explicit category/delete operations.
- `SavedSubQuestion` now carries Type_CQ category IDs for edit validation. `TypeCqQuestionRepositoryImpl.loadOriginal` reads those links when loading a group for editing.
- `TypeCqQuestionDao.insertGroup` inserts new category links in its existing transaction, validates active category IDs, and reads category links back before draft cleanup.
- `TypeCqQuestionDao.updateGroup` validates every affected record against the exact `(topic_id, stimulus_id)` scope; validates category assignments; replaces only explicitly supplied category sets; deletes only explicitly named questions in the target stimulus group; verifies deletion and category read-back; then clears drafts as the last transactional step. Any check/insert failure throws within the Room transaction and rolls back the relational save and draft cleanup.
- `ChapterCqRepositoryImpl.saveQuestionWithStimulus` creates a defensive bundle snapshot before delegating to the existing `@Transaction` DAO save. Existing Chapter_CQ ownership checks and read-back remain in the DAO.

71.3 Existing safeguards inspected and retained
- Type_CQ New/Edit ViewModels set their save-in-flight state before starting asynchronous work and refuse a second save while that state is active.
- Chapter_CQ editor uses `savingQuestion` as an in-flight guard.
- Type_CQ create/edit and Chapter_CQ bundle writes remain under existing Room `@Transaction` DAO methods.
- Success follows the DAO's read-back checks; there is no second repository read-back after commit. Existing question IDs are retained for updated rows.
- Type_CQ text-only solution updates continue to preserve existing non-TEXT solution parts.

71.4 Remaining work and known limits
- The Type_CQ Edit UI does not yet expose delete-question or category-management controls. Persistence command fields and DAO operations are implemented; user-facing controls remain to be wired with Phase 7/11.

============================================================ 72. PHASE 9 TYPE_CQ EDIT UI INTEGRATION (v1.63)
============================================================

72.1 Owner direction and verification boundary
- Continue the v1.62 Phase 9 implementation and complete the next stated task: connect Type_CQ explicit deletion and category persistence to the Edit screen.
- The prior instruction not to run tests/builds remains in force. No Gradle build, Kotlin/KSP/Room compilation, unit test, instrumented test, APK build/signing, emulator run or device run was performed. This update is source-only and statically inspected.
- Phase 9 remains PARTIAL / WRITTEN / UNVERIFIED. Source presence and static inspection do not establish compilation or runtime success.
- No Room schema, database version, migration, versionCode or versionName was changed. App metadata remains versionCode=36 and versionName="0.2.31-phase5-wip2".

72.2 Type_CQ Edit UI integration written
- The Edit screen now loads the live Type_CQ category catalog from the existing TypeCqData observation flow.
- Each question block with nonblank question text exposes category checkboxes. Active categories can be assigned. An already-linked archived category remains visible; it may be removed but cannot be newly assigned.
- A per-question category-management action navigates to the existing Type_CQ Category reference catalog, where categories can be added, renamed, archived or reassigned. Returning to Edit observes catalog changes through the existing Flow.
- The Edit ViewModel initializes category selections from saved links and sends complete per-label category sets to GroupEditInput. The existing transactional DAO code from v1.62 performs link replacement and read-back.
- The canonical schema-v2 edit draft now carries category selections, so category-only edits survive draft restore.

72.3 Explicit question deletion UI
- For a saved question block inside a stimulus group, Edit exposes a confirmation-gated "এই প্রশ্নটি মুছুন" action.
- At least one question must remain. A newly filled question block counts as a replacement candidate; a non-stimulus single-question edit does not expose this group-delete action.
- Pending deletions are visibly listed before save and can be cancelled individually. The deleted IDs flow into GroupEditInput and the v1.62 immutable EditorSaveCommand/Room transaction path.
- The Edit undo/redo snapshot now includes category IDs and deleted-question IDs, so these operations participate in editor history.

72.4 Draft-writer race and recovery handling
- The application-scope Edit draft queue now carries one immutable payload containing text, category IDs, deleted-question IDs and editor mode. The worker no longer reads category/deletion selections from mutable UI state after dequeue.
- Canonical draft restore loads category selections for all labels, including a block temporarily marked for deletion, and restores pending deleted IDs. This allows the person to cancel a deletion without losing the rest of that block's pending draft.
- The save-failure branch writes the same captured text/category/deletion snapshot back to the canonical draft store.

72.5 Changed source files in v1.63
- `app/src/main/java/com/questionbank/app/ui/question/QuestionEditScreen.kt` — category checkboxes, category-management navigation action, question-delete confirmation and pending-delete cancellation UI.
- `app/src/main/java/com/questionbank/app/ui/question/QuestionEditViewModel.kt` — category catalog/state, complete category payloads, explicit deletion actions, history snapshots and immutable application-scope draft snapshots.
- `app/src/main/java/com/questionbank/app/ui/navigation/AppNavHost.kt` — category catalog flow and Category Management route wiring.

72.6 Remaining work and limits
- Phase 9 acceptance remains unverified until duplicate-save, forced-failure/rollback and save/reload evidence is produced. These tests/builds were not run in this update because of the owner instruction.
- Chapter_CQ remains a single-question-block bundle editor; multi-question-block group management/deletion is not implemented here.
- Filesystem media writes are not part of the Room transaction. Phase 10 must coordinate image file operations, operation journal state, verification, recovery and orphan cleanup.
- No APK is included. This ZIP is a source-only implementation candidate and is not a tested release.

72.7 Next step
- Preserve Phase 9 as PARTIAL / WRITTEN / UNVERIFIED until build/runtime and planned duplicate-save/forced-failure evidence is authorised and available. Then continue with the Phase 10 image/media pipeline without claiming filesystem operations are atomic with Room.

============================================================ 74. SECOND STATIC RECHECK AND SAFETY CORRECTIONS (v1.65)
============================================================

Planning update date: 20 November 2026
Recheck baseline: QuestionBank_PHASE10_12_v1_64_SOURCE_UNVERIFIED.zip
Candidate: QuestionBank_PHASE10_12_v1_65_RECHECKED_SOURCE_UNVERIFIED.zip

74.1 Verification boundary
- The owner previously instructed that tests/builds should not be run. That instruction remains in force.
- Rechecked the v1.64 archive integrity, source-tree delta against v1.63, Phase 10 media operations/recovery, Type_CQ and Chapter_CQ deletion paths, Type_CQ filter flow, navigation wiring, app/database metadata boundaries and checklist consistency.
- No Gradle build, Kotlin/KSP/Room compilation, unit test, instrumented test, APK assembly, emulator run or device run was performed.
- This is a static source review only. It cannot establish compilation, Room processing, database runtime behavior, file-system crash recovery or UI behavior.

74.2 Findings and source corrections
1. Media orphan cleanup previously traversed the complete `filesDir/media` tree and treated every path not present in the Type_CQ/Chapter_CQ image tables as orphaned. That could remove a future Paper media namespace. The collector now owns only `media/type_cq` and `media/chapter_cq`, while paper/other namespaces are left untouched. Import staging cleanup is limited to `media/.pending` and preserves IDs with pending image-journal operations.
2. Media recovery previously iterated every pending shared operation and could mark an unrelated operation as FAILED if it happened to use a Type_CQ/Chapter_CQ scope. Recovery now handles only `IMAGE_IMPORT`, `IMAGE_TRANSFORM` and `IMAGE_DELETE` operation types.
3. The QuestionCard delete confirmation counted only currently displayed subquestions. In a filtered search result the repository deletes the complete original group, not just the visible subset. The message now explicitly says the whole card/group will be deleted.
4. Media-library deletion was previously immediate. A confirmation dialog is now required before the irreversible delete request; the original reference guard remains in the pipeline.
5. The image-picker callback previously used the currently selected scope on return. Changing scope while the picker was open could place the imported image in a different scope from the one at launch. The UI now captures the import scope when opening the picker.
6. Media import/transform/export/delete controls remain unavailable until pending-operation recovery and reconciliation complete successfully when the media library opens.

74.3 Source files corrected in this recheck
- `app/src/main/java/com/questionbank/app/media/ImagePipeline.kt` — scoped orphan collection, protection of pending journal-owned files and operation-type filtering.
- `app/src/main/java/com/questionbank/app/ui/media/ImageLibraryScreen.kt` — captured picker scope, recovery-ready gating and delete confirmation.
- `app/src/main/java/com/questionbank/app/ui/question/QuestionCard.kt` — accurate full-group delete confirmation wording for normal and filtered search cards.
- `PROJECT_PLAN_v1_65_RECHECKED.txt` — master checklist and latest status reconciled with v1.63–v1.65 changes.

74.4 Reconciled status
- Phase 9 remains PARTIAL / WRITTEN / UNVERIFIED; duplicate-save and forced-failure/rollback evidence is still open.
- Phase 10 remains PARTIAL / WRITTEN / UNVERIFIED; editor attachment/linking, TOP/BOTTOM placement, ordering, replacement, unlinking, interactive crop, camera, startup recovery scheduling, paper asset retention and media backup/restore remain open.
- Phase 11 remains PARTIAL / WRITTEN / UNVERIFIED; detail screen, copy/cut/paste, multi-select, bulk operations, reorder/sort/pagination and Chapter_CQ multi-block group management remain open.
- Phase 12 remains PARTIAL / WRITTEN / UNVERIFIED; global cross-scope and Chapter_CQ search/filter, complete Bengali/English acceptance coverage and performance verification remain open.
- Canonical SQL, Room entities/schema JSON, database version, identityHash, versionCode and versionName remain unchanged. App metadata remains versionCode=36 and versionName="0.2.31-phase5-wip2".

74.5 Change log
- v1.65: second static review corrected media cleanup ownership boundaries, media recovery operation filtering, media delete confirmation, import-scope capture and full-group delete warning. No tests/build run; Phases 9–12 remain unverified.

75.2 Phase 10 — Image and Media Pipeline (PARTIAL / SOURCE IMPLEMENTED / UNVERIFIED)
Source work added or wired:
- Type_CQ new-question and Edit flows now carry stimulus image references and mixed ordered TEXT/IMAGE solution parts through EditorDraft, ViewModel snapshots, immutable save commands, repository mapping and DAO transaction/read-back paths.
- Chapter_CQ editor snapshots and durable draft serialization now retain stimulus image references and mixed solution parts; the Chapter_CQ save bundle writes stimulus-image links and solution-part rows through its existing transaction boundary with validation/read-back.
- Editor image controls import through the existing SAF/OpenDocument media pipeline into app-private storage. The selected TYPE_CQ or CHAPTER_CQ scope is captured when the picker is opened, rather than being read from a later mutable selection when the result returns.
- Stimulus image controls expose TOP/BOTTOM placement, ordering, replacement and unlink/removal. Solution controls expose image attachment, removal, replacement and ordering across the combined TEXT/IMAGE solution-part sequence.
- Image preview thumbnails resolve through the scope-specific media path. Import failures are surfaced in the Editor instead of being silently ignored; coroutine cancellation is propagated.
- Type_CQ newly added sub-question blocks in an Edit transaction can persist ordered mixed solution parts rather than being treated as already-existing question IDs.
- Existing v1.65 safeguards remain: scope-limited orphan-file collection, image-operation-only journal recovery, picker-scope capture, recovery-ready control gating, reference-guarded media deletion and delete confirmation.

Still open in Phase 10:
- Stimulus and solution image rows now have a Replace control that imports a new asset and swaps the attachment ID in the draft. The previously linked asset is not automatically deleted; safe cleanup/retention of unowned metadata remains open.
- Type_CQ Edit does not yet create a new stimulus group for an existing standalone question that previously had no stimulus. New-question creation and Chapter_CQ's current editor can create an image-only stimulus.
- Camera capture and an interactive crop UI remain open; current crop/rotate behavior is the existing fixed crop/rotation path.
- Application-startup recovery scheduling and comprehensive process-death/filesystem lifecycle proof remain open.
- Paper-asset ownership/retention, media archive backup/restore, and cleanup of unowned metadata rows remain open. Filesystem and Room writes are not claimed to be one atomic transaction.

75.3 Phase 11 — Question Cards and Question Management (PARTIAL / SOURCE IMPLEMENTED / UNVERIFIED)
Source work added or wired:
- Type_CQ has a dedicated question-detail screen that loads the selected card/group and displays stimulus, stimulus images, ordered solution content and question metadata before navigation to Edit.
- Chapter_CQ has a detail dialog displaying stimulus, stimulus images, ordered solution parts, board/year information, categories and marks/rating.
- Type_CQ supports full-group duplicate/copy, including stimulus data, image links, ordered solution parts, board/year tags and category links, using newly created question/solution identifiers while reusing scope-owned image assets.
- Type_CQ bulk delete resolves the complete selected groups, validates scope and membership, and delegates to one Room transaction. It does not delete only the currently visible sub-questions from a filtered card. Restrictive references can reject the transaction rather than being bypassed.
- Chapter_CQ supports question-level duplicate/copy and multi-select batch delete. The batch DAO checks chapter ownership and uses a transaction; restrictive references can reject the batch.
- Type_CQ and Chapter_CQ list surfaces expose selection and sorting by rating, marks and question type/label. Type_CQ displays results in 40-item increments using an explicit load-more action.

Still open in Phase 11:
- Chapter_CQ remains a question-level workflow; complete multi-block stimulus-group editing, duplicate/delete and group-level management are not implemented.
- Cut/paste and a full clipboard workflow are not implemented. The existing copy action is duplicate/copy, not a move operation.
- Chapter_CQ list pagination and database-backed Paging 3 remain open. Type_CQ's 40-item increment is UI-side over its currently loaded collection, not proof of database paging scalability.
- Full card/detail UI acceptance and large-dataset behavior remain unverified.

75.4 Phase 12 — Search, Filters and Duplicate Detection (PARTIAL / SOURCE IMPLEMENTED / UNVERIFIED)
Source work added or wired:
- A separate global search domain model, repository, use case, ViewModel and Filter-tab UI now query both existing FTS5 virtual tables through a read-only Room reader connection.
- The result query keeps Type_CQ and Chapter_CQ table families separate and combines them as a cross-scope result projection; it does not add cross-scope DAO calls to either domain repository.
- Text search uses each scope's FTS5 index with a bounded LIKE fallback for stimulus text and hashtag terms. Scope can be ALL, TYPE_CQ or CHAPTER_CQ.
- Board, examination year, category (with an explicit category scope), rating and Type_CQ question-type filters can be combined with text search. Question-type filtering is disabled for Chapter_CQ.
- Results display scope, subject/chapter/topic-or-chapter path, stimulus, question, short answer, marks/rating, board/year tags and categories. Type_CQ results navigate to the dedicated question detail screen; Chapter_CQ results open its detail dialog.
- A duplicate indicator compares exact lowercased/trimmed question text across the two question tables, excluding the current row only within its own scope. It is an exact-text warning, not fuzzy/similarity matching.
- The global-search ViewModel uses a generation guard to prevent an older cancelled query from overwriting a newer filter result or loading state.
- Static corrections also handle the rating fallback for questions without a stimulus and cross-scope duplicate matching when independently scoped IDs happen to coincide.

Still open in Phase 12:
- Duplicate comparison is lower/trim exact-text matching and does not implement full Unicode canonical normalization or fuzzy similarity. Bengali/Latin digit normalization remains unresolved as documented earlier.
- Search result highlighting, more advanced sorting, and result counts/pagination beyond the bounded 300-result projection remain open.
- Bengali and English tokenizer behavior, query punctuation/whitespace cases, FTS rebuild behavior and performance on real datasets have not been tested in this update.
- The global search query is source-written only; actual Room reader-connection and FTS runtime behavior remains unverified.

75.5 Static review notes and corrections in v1.66
- New Type_CQ Editor text-solution operations preserve existing image solution parts rather than rebuilding the solution list as text-only rows.
- The Type_CQ Edit repository/DAO path treats newly added sub-question rows separately from updates to existing row IDs and writes their ordered solution parts as part of the group transaction.
- Chapter_CQ solution text synchronization preserves IMAGE parts and maintains the combined solution order.
- Image picker scope is captured at launch in both Editor attachment controls.
- Global search category filtering is scoped to the chosen category family; board/year predicates include question and stimulus tags; stimulus-less question rating does not aggregate unrelated NULL-stimulus rows.
- Cross-scope duplicate comparison no longer excludes a row in the other scope merely because its ID text matches the current-scope ID.
- These are source-level corrections only. No claim is made that they compile or pass runtime behavior.

- Custom paper-category selection/management, section deletion, full paper-library management and database-backed paging for large source lists remain open.
- PDF/print/export is not implemented; the plan says export/print only if approved, and no additional export format was approved in this update.
- Snapshot schema v1 is written/read; a future snapshot migration path beyond the current version is not implemented.
- No source-deletion, source-edit, image-retention or ordering persistence acceptance evidence was produced. Phase 13 exit criteria remain UNVERIFIED.

76.3 Phase 14 — Backup and Restore (SOURCE UPDATED / PARTIAL / UNVERIFIED)
Source work added or wired:
- Manual backup now writes a `.qbbak` ZIP archive containing a database snapshot plus committed image files under `media/type_cq`, `media/chapter_cq` and `paper_media`.
- `manifest.json` records archive format version, creation time, application version/versionCode, database version, Room identity hash, database size/SHA-256 and a path/size/SHA-256 inventory for every included media file.
- Restore extraction rejects unlisted paths, duplicate paths, traversal paths, unexpected content, missing files, hash/length mismatches and archives exceeding configured per-entry/total extraction limits.
- Restore preview displays database row counts and whether media assets are included. The confirmed archive is staged before the app closes the database.
- Startup restore swap runs in `Application.onCreate` before the lazy Room database can be opened. The previous database and both media trees are retained as `.replaced` paths; a marker allows interrupted-swap recovery to prefer the previous consistent set.
- Legacy raw database-only `.db` backups remain readable. They leave current media files unchanged rather than pretending the old backup contains media.

Still open in Phase 14:
- Automatic backup scheduling/policy was not approved or implemented. Background BackupWorker/RestoreWorker orchestration is not complete; the current user-facing workflow is manual and uses BackupService plus startup restore swapping.
- Media mutation/backup mutual exclusion is not yet enforced by one shared snapshot lock. A concurrent image operation during archive creation remains a consistency risk to be closed before declaring Phase 14 complete.
- Cross-device restore, interrupted restore, corrupted archive/media and low-storage behavior have not been exercised.
- Backup encryption, cloud transport and retention/rotation policy are not included.
- Existing `.db` compatibility is source-written only; no restore scenario was run. Phase 14 remains UNVERIFIED.

76.4 ADR update — JPEG storage policy (accepted for this source candidate; schema-neutral)
- The earlier project text specified WebP, 800 px and quality 0.8. The source baseline instead used JPEG, a 2048 px maximum edge and quality 88. This mismatch is now explicitly recorded rather than silently rewriting the old rule.
- Decision for this candidate: retain JPEG, maximum edge 2048 px and quality 88 for new image imports/transforms. Reason: preserve legibility of small text, diagrams and question-paper figures; a lower 800 px ceiling could reduce their readability.
- Export converts the stored image to PNG. Existing decodable JPEG/PNG/WebP files remain supported as sources; this decision does not convert old files in place.
- The backup archive includes supported image extensions and preserves their stored bytes. The decision is recorded in `docs/Architecture_Decision_Records.md` as ADR-013.

76.5 Media recovery architecture and startup integration
- Added separate source components: `ImageIntegrityChecker`, `ImageReferenceResolver`, `OrphanMediaCollector` and `ImageRecoveryWorker`.
- `ImagePipeline` remains the application-facing façade and delegates integrity, safe-reference deletion and orphan collection to those components.
- WorkManager schedules unique retryable startup recovery after restore-swap handling and container creation. The image library also awaits the same recovery gate before enabling media mutations.
- Image imports, transforms, question-image deletion, paper-image copy and paper-image deletion await successful recovery. Paper media remains outside the question-scope collector roots.
- This is a source-level integration. WorkManager scheduling, process-death recovery, concurrent operation ordering and every filesystem/Room failure case remain unverified.

76.6 Phase 15 — Settings, Accessibility and Polish (SOURCE UPDATED / PARTIAL / UNVERIFIED)
Source work added or wired:
- Profile settings now include a default editor mode (Minimum / Full) and a search-term highlighting preference.
- The selected default mode is wired to new Type_CQ questions, Type_CQ Edit and Chapter_CQ question creation; a stored canonical draft mode takes precedence when it exists.
- Global Search can highlight matched terms when the owner enables the preference.
- Existing theme and text-size controls remain in Profile. The image-storage policy is described there for transparency.
- Added accessible content descriptions to selected controls and Paper Builder selection affordances; existing Material text labels remain available.

Still open in Phase 15:
- These preferences remain in SharedPreferences and are not included in database/media backup archives.
- No configurable image-quality controls, full keyboard-navigation/focus-order audit, comprehensive screen-reader audit, touch-target audit or responsive small/large-screen review was completed.
- Font-file integration, broad dialog/snackbar polish, image-loading profiling, memory/performance profiling and accessibility report remain open.
- No UI interaction or accessibility acceptance was run. Phase 15 remains PARTIAL / UNVERIFIED.

- Paper Builder is routed from the Create tab and uses the PaperRepository supplied by AppContainer.
- The `.qbbak` manual backup and staged restore path is exposed through the Profile backup screen; the startup restore swap is invoked before the Room container is constructed.
- Image recovery is scheduled at application startup through unique WorkManager work and shares the same ImagePipeline recovery gate with the media library and paper-media operations.
- Default editor mode and search highlighting preferences are passed from AppContainer into their relevant screens/ViewModels.
- Static source review corrections addressed the backup result/preview model fields, the edit ViewModel default-editor-mode constructor wiring, invalid scope-qualified search selectors, paper-media deletion gating and restore rollback of originally empty media roots.

Not completed in Phase 16:
- No unit/Room/migration/repository/editor/media/search/paper/backup/UI/process-death/low-memory/large-dataset/cross-scope tests were run.
- The 146-item Editor audit was not executed and no release-candidate build was produced.
- No 146-item traceability completion report, executed defect-resolution report or regression pass report is claimed.

77. CAREFUL SOURCE RECHECK — VERSION 1.68 HOTFIX CANDIDATE
============================================================

Recheck date: 20 November 2026
Base package: QuestionBank_PHASE13_16_v1_67_SOURCE_UNVERIFIED.zip
Updated package: QuestionBank_PHASE13_16_v1_68_RECHECKED_SOURCE_UNVERIFIED.zip
Scope: Static source and archive inspection only. No Gradle build, compile,
KSP/Room generation, test, emulator/device run or APK assembly.

77.1 v1.67 package review
- The v1.67 ZIP archive was readable and its internal entry checks passed.
  Its SHA-256 matched the supplied checksum file. This establishes archive
  integrity only; it is not evidence that Android source compiles or runs.
- Compared with v1.66, 38 paths were added or changed; no source paths were
  deleted in the package comparison.
- Canonical SQL, Room exported schema JSON, Room database registration,
  database version, Room identity hash, app versionCode and versionName
  were unchanged by the v1.67 source update.

77.2 Critical defect found and source correction applied in v1.68
The v1.67 RestoreSwap rollback path ignored Boolean results from File.renameTo
(and deletion operations), then removed the recovery marker unconditionally.
The interrupted-restore recovery path also ignored rollback operation results
and removed the marker even if the previous database/media pair could not be
restored. The Application startup path would then continue toward AppContainer
and could allow Room to open after incomplete recovery.

The v1.68 source candidate changes this behavior as follows:
1. WAL/SHM sidecars are moved before the main database so a crash between
   moves can be identified and reversed independently.
2. Rollback verifies every old database, sidecar and media restoration step.
3. The recovery marker is retained when any rollback operation fails.
4. Startup returns a recoveryRequired failure and refuses to initialize the
   Room container while the marker indicates incomplete recovery.
5. The marker is fsynced before live paths are moved.
6. Any rollback-failure recovery remains retryable on a later process start.

This is a source-level corrective change only. No fault-injection test, file
permission test, process-death test or device restore test was run.

77.3 Remaining high-priority Phase 14 gaps
- BackupArchive calculates media file length/SHA-256 while enumerating files,
  then opens those source paths again later to add them to the ZIP. There is no
  shared snapshot mutex coordinating this with ImagePipeline imports,
  transforms, deletes and paper-image copy/delete operations. A mutation in
  that interval can make the ZIP payload differ from the manifest record.
- BackupService verifies that the user-selected output bytes equal the
  internally generated ZIP bytes, but it does not re-extract and validate
  the finished ZIP before reporting Done. Thus an internally inconsistent
  manifest/archive could be reported as exported, then be rejected on restore.
- No BackupWorker or RestoreWorker exists in app source. Automatic backup
  policy and background worker orchestration remain unimplemented.
- Backup preview counts are Type_CQ-only (DbCounts); Chapter_CQ data is in
  the database archive but is not represented in the displayed count summary.
- Restore crash/fault scenarios and media/database consistency remain
  unverified.

77.4 Remaining Phase 13 gaps
- Paper Builder source path is partial: source selection is limited to the
  first 80 rows and is not database-paged.
- Preview is text-only; paper-owned image assets are not rendered there.
- Full per-item override editing/override-aware preview, section deletion,
  library management, category management and approved PDF/print/export
  are not complete.
- Paper orphan collection removes files for unreferenced image rows; it does
  not scan paper_media for files left behind before a database row was
  committed (for example, a process death between file copy and DB write).

77.5 Remaining Phase 15 gaps
- Theme and text-size preferences exist; v1.67 adds default editor mode and
  search-term highlighting.
- Configurable image behavior, font integration, complete keyboard/focus
  order, screen-reader semantics, touch-target review, responsive layouts,
  and broad interaction/performance polish remain open.
- No accessibility audit or UI acceptance was performed.

77.6 Phase 16 status
Phase 16 is SOURCE INTEGRATION REVIEW ONLY / TESTS NOT RUN / NOT COMPLETED.
The required regression suite, 146-item Editor audit, defect-resolution
evidence and release-candidate build have not been produced. The owner has
explicitly instructed that no tests be run; therefore Phase 16 cannot be
marked passed or completed against the current plan's exit criteria.

77.7 Final status after careful recheck
- Phase 13: SOURCE UPDATED / PARTIAL / UNVERIFIED.
- Phase 14: SOURCE UPDATED / PARTIAL / UNVERIFIED; the v1.67 critical
  rollback defect is corrected in v1.68 source, while archive snapshot
  concurrency and worker/policy gaps remain.
- Phase 15: SOURCE UPDATED / PARTIAL / UNVERIFIED.
- Phase 16: SOURCE INTEGRATION REVIEW ONLY / TESTS NOT RUN / NOT COMPLETED.
- Phase 17: NOT STARTED.
- No build, compile, tests, APK assembly or runtime verification is claimed.

============================================================ 79. v1.70 FINAL STATIC RECHECK — SOURCE ONLY
============================================================

79.1 Recovery-gate lock ordering correction
- Static inspection identified a deadlock possibility when a media mutation acquired
  MediaSnapshotCoordinator and then awaited ImagePipeline.recoveryGate, while startup
  recovery needed the same coordinator lock to complete that gate.
- Updated importImage, transformImage, deleteImage, exportImage, copyImageToPaperStorage,
  deletePaperStorageAsset and collectUntrackedPaperFiles so they await recovery readiness
  BEFORE acquiring the shared media snapshot/mutation lock.
- runStartupRecovery continues to hold the coordinator lock while replaying the journal and
  reconciling managed folders, then completes the gate. No media mutation can enter before
  recovery succeeds. Read-only verification/file lookup methods await the gate without
  acquiring the coordinator lock.
- This is a source-level lock-order correction only. It has not been concurrency-tested.

79.2 Paper mark validation hardening
- updatePaperMetadata and updateItemMarks reject NaN, positive/negative infinity and negative
  mark values before writing.
- validatePaper reports non-finite item or declared total marks instead of allowing NaN to
  contaminate the sum or bypass the declared-total comparison.
- No database constraint, table, Room Entity or schema version was changed.

79.3 Final static checks permitted in this package
- Source inspection confirms every ImagePipeline mutation/export path awaits recovery before
  entering MediaSnapshotCoordinator; runStartupRecovery is the only path that takes that
  lock before opening the gate.
- Paper and backup source paths were re-read for call-site wiring and documented limitations.
- Canonical SQL and exported Room schema remain unchanged from v1.68; database version stays 1.
- App versionCode remains 36 and versionName remains 0.2.31-phase5-wip2.

83.2 Source changes in this candidate
Question card and question list:
- QuestionCard.kt: larger rounded card and blue ribbon; question number, selection checkbox, five-star yellow rating badge and overflow action; pale board/year strip; expanded typography/spacing; question/solution divider and bordered solution toggle; preserved image display and solution expansion.

- EditorImageAttachments.kt: compact square attachment controls in the stimulus/solution toolbar while preserving the existing image import, replace, reorder and remove behavior.
- QuestionEditorScreen.kt and QuestionEditScreen.kt: matching stimulus/question/solution sections, larger text fields and compact attachments in both editor modes; type/marks controls and math bar remain Full-mode capabilities where already supported; existing preview, answer, draft, undo/redo, validation and save/update behavior retained.

83.3 Existing app capabilities intentionally retained / visual parity limits
- The new app enters the Type_CQ editor after a topic has already been selected in the app's hierarchy navigation. A duplicate subject/chapter/topic picker was not added inside the editor, because the current editor ViewModel/route is topic-scoped and does not own hierarchy selection state.
- The screenshot's JSON Import and folder import/export menu actions are not added to the question-list overflow: those workflows are not exposed as complete Type_CQ list actions in the current target screen. No fake menu entries are added.
- Cut, Paste, Paste before and Paste after are not added to the card menu because the current Type_CQ list has no corresponding clipboard/reorder API.
- Android Compose controls cannot be pixel-identical to the browser-rendered CAAP reference. Sizes, color, order, text hierarchy and interaction layout are approximated natively.
- The target still has a plain-text math preview and append-at-end symbol insertion; full KaTeX/MathJax rendering and cursor-aware equation editing are not part of this update.
