85. CAA EDITOR FUNCTIONAL-PARITY PASS — VERSION 1.76
============================================================

Candidate archive: QuestionBank_v1_76_CAA_EDITOR_PARITY.zip
Base: v1.75 CAAP UI compile-fix source candidate.
Reference source: CAA_EDITOR_FEATURES_Minimum_Full.txt (copied into the project root; 27-section reference for Minimum/Full Editor behavior).
Status: SOURCE CANDIDATE ONLY — NOT COMPILED OR TESTED.

85.1 Scope and intent
- Use the uploaded CAA Editor reference as the required behavior and UI reference for the native Android Minimum and Full Editor.
- Implement the first functional-parity increment on existing Jetpack Compose Type_CQ create/edit screens and ViewModels.
- Preserve the existing persistence model and transactional write paths; do not claim full CAA parity while structural and visual requirements remain open.
- CAA's source React/TypeScript implementation is a behavioral reference only; the target remains native Android Compose.

85.2 Implemented in this source pass
- QuestionEditorViewModel now initializes a new Type_CQ question group with only the CAA default two visible blocks: গ (3 marks, rating 3) and ঘ (4 marks, rating 4). The default solution field for each is initialized as one blank text part.
- QuestionEditorScreen renders those initial two blocks and presents the block count only in Full mode.
- Full mode provides an add-block action for the first unused ক/খ/গ/ঘ label and a delete action with destructive confirmation. The UI will not remove the last visible block.
- New-form Full mode provides a block-label dropdown and marks input. Reassignment moves text, short answer, marks, rating, board/year tags, solution text and mixed ordered solution parts together to an unused label. If marks still equal the previous label default, the new label default is applied; otherwise the user's custom marks are retained.
- QuestionEditViewModel and QuestionEditScreen can add a missing label on a stimulus group, and can remove a pending new label or mark a persisted block for deletion after confirmation. Persisted deletion continues to occur only through the existing transactional group update.
- Text field row counts now match the reference: stimulus Minimum/Full = 2/4; question Minimum/Full = 2/3; solution Minimum/Full = 2/3.
- Restored content is preserved while editor presentation mode resets to the default Minimum mode on each Type_CQ create/edit form open. Chapter_CQ draft restore and new form open also reset the editor mode to its configured default (Minimum).
- EditorDesign.kt receives an initial visual refinement for the reference segmented mode selector, card padding, section title sizing, 32dp eye control, 36dp header actions and dark live-preview surface.
- Existing board/year controls, rating controls, image import/replace/reorder/remove paths, ordered solution parts, draft writer, undo/redo, duplicate warning and atomic save/update paths remain the underlying implementation.

85.3 Critical model limitation — do not overstate support
- The current Type_CQ legacy DraftText / TypeCqDraftAdapter model remains a fixed four-slot array indexed by CqLabel (ক/খ/গ/ঘ).
- One question block per label is supported. The Full-mode UI therefore exposes only unused labels and reports this limitation after the four labels are used.
- Repeated blocks with the same label, arbitrary unbounded block counts and independent block ordering are NOT implemented. Enabling them requires an intentional domain/storage/adapter migration and data-preservation tests; this is not a cosmetic UI-only change.
- Changing the saved label of an existing persisted question is not yet supported in the edit workflow. Full mode still edits the existing question-type metadata and marks through the legacy edit control; new-question block reassignment is limited to unused labels.
- The current canonical/legacy save model and Room SQL were not changed in this pass.

85.4 CAA requirements still outstanding
- Full hierarchy picker card inside the editor (subject/chapter/topic), including auto-switch to Full and scroll-to-first-error. The current Type_CQ editor route already receives a topicId and does not own the hierarchy navigation state.
- A complete KaTeX renderer for inline `$...$` and block `$$...$$` math, graceful formula errors and actual typeset previews. Current preview remains plain text.
- Cursor/selection-aware math toolbar insertion, CAA's nine toolbar actions/Greek palette and the custom textarea resize grip. Existing symbol insertion still appends at the end.
- Full CAA image edit queue after selection (rotate, four-side crop, grayscale/black-white scan, settings toggle), precise image control layout, in-editor large zoom/pan viewer and complete image interaction semantics. The existing pipeline has import, replace, reorder, remove, integrity/recovery, PNG export from image library and separate rotate/center-crop operations; those are not the same as the CAA workflow.
- CAA-compatible `[[img:<id>]]` / `[[box]]` solution marker serialization, parser and legacy backup round-trip. Native app currently stores normalized ordered TEXT/IMAGE solution parts.
- Exact CAA header dirty badge, all confirmation-dialog layouts/strings, accessibility labels/tooltips, toast text, save-time validation auto-navigation and full duplicate confirmation content.
- A true shared grouped Minimum/Full editor for Chapter_CQ. Chapter_CQ remains its existing separate single-question workflow; this pass only aligns its mode-reset behavior.
- Complete CAA 27-section acceptance checklist and visual device comparison.

85.5 Files changed / added

- app/src/main/java/com/questionbank/app/ui/question/QuestionEditViewModel.kt
- app/src/main/java/com/questionbank/app/ui/question/QuestionEditorScreen.kt

============================================================
86. CAA EDITOR EDIT-MODE EDGE-CASE CORRECTION — VERSION 1.77
============================================================

Candidate archive: QuestionBank_v1_77_CAA_EDITOR_PARITY.zip
Base: QuestionBank_v1_76_CAA_EDITOR_PARITY.zip
Reference: CAA_EDITOR_FEATURES_Minimum_Full.txt
Status: SOURCE CANDIDATE ONLY — ANDROID BUILD NOT VERIFIED.

86.1 Reason for this increment
- The v1.76 static recheck found that Edit-mode Add could choose a label whose persisted row had only been marked for deletion. Because the row is not actually removed until the transactional save commits, the add handler could update draft arrays without the label becoming visible.
- The delete eligibility calculation also counted a pending new block only when its question text was nonblank. That could prevent removing another saved row even though a second visible editor block already existed and save-time validation would handle its blank required text.

86.2 Changes made
- Added the pure domain helper `QuestionEditBlockRules` for the legacy one-row-per-ক/খ/গ/ঘ rules.
- `QuestionEditViewModel.addableLabels` now includes only labels that have no persisted row and are not already visible as pending blocks. A persisted row remains occupied throughout the edit session even after the user marks it for deletion.
- `prepareQuestionBlock` checks the same `addableLabels` rule, so correctness does not depend only on the Compose UI.
- `QuestionEditScreen` uses the ViewModel's addable-label list, disables Add if there is no genuinely addable label, and explains why a deleted persisted label cannot be re-added before save.
- The persisted-question deletion guard now counts all visible pending blocks, including a block whose required text is still blank. Required-text validation remains part of Save and a blank block cannot be committed as a valid question.
- Added four targeted pure unit tests for occupied/deleted labels, visible pending labels, pending-block-aware delete eligibility and negative counts.

86.3 Files changed / added
- `app/src/main/java/com/questionbank/app/domain/question/QuestionEditBlockRules.kt` (new)

86.4 Compatibility / scope boundary
- No SQL, Room Entity, DAO, migration, database version, dependency, Gradle plugin, `versionCode` or `versionName` change.
- The current Type_CQ data adapter still supports only one row per CQ label. This increment intentionally does not support re-adding a just-deleted persisted label in the same transaction; doing so safely needs an explicit delete-plus-insert domain operation and compatibility tests.
- Repeated same-label blocks, arbitrary block ordering, saved-label reassignment, shared Chapter_CQ grouped editor, KaTeX, cursor-aware math insertion, CAA image edit queue/viewer, CAA solution marker import/export, hierarchy selection/error navigation and complete 27-section acceptance remain open.

86.5 Verification record
- Reviewed the source diff and the Edit-mode state / add / delete paths.
- Compiled and executed the pure `QuestionEditBlockRules` helper with an isolated Kotlin compiler/run harness; the targeted assertions passed. This is not the Gradle/JUnit app test task.
- Android Gradle attempt remains blocked before project tasks: Gradle 8.14.3 is not cached locally and `services.gradle.org` DNS resolution fails. Kotlin/Compose compile, KSP/Room generation, Gradle unit tests, androidTest compile, debug/release APK assembly and emulator/physical-device tests are NOT VERIFIED for v1.77.
- ZIP CRC/integrity is checked separately; it does not establish that Android source compiles.

86.6 Required next gates
1. Run CI on the exact v1.77 source candidate with Gradle distribution access.
2. Verify `compileDebugKotlin`, KSP/Room generation, `testDebugUnitTest`, androidTest compilation and `assembleRelease`.
3. Test: delete a persisted block, add a different never-persisted label, attempt to add the deleted label (must remain unavailable), delete a persisted block while a visible new blank block exists, fill it, save, reopen and verify; also test discard/reopen and failed-save rollback.
4. Before repeated same-label blocks are enabled, design the generalized block model, schema migration, adapter/backward compatibility and data-preservation fixtures.
5. Continue the remaining CAA reference implementation; Phase 6, Phase 7, Phase 8 and CAA parity are not marked DONE by this increment.
